
STOCK_TYPE_EXPL(SH,SE)=0;
MATRICE_TYPE_EXPL(ST,SE)=0;

NB_BOUCLE=0;


LOOP(SH,


*ooooooo Calcul des charges variables par OTK puis par culture (en euros /ha/an)
         CV_HA_OTK_Cult(SC,SK)=
                  (       Matrice_OTK_Cult(SK, SC)$(Data_OTK(SK,"AMORTI")=0)*Data_OTK(SK,"DOSE")*Data_OTK(SK,"PRIX_UNIT")
                       + (Matrice_OTK_Cult(SK, SC)$(Data_OTK(SK,"AMORTI")=1)*Data_OTK(SK,"DOSE")*Data_OTK(SK,"PRIX_UNIT") )/Duree_Plant_Cult(SC,SH)
                   )/ Duree_Cycle_Cult(SC,SH)*12;
         STOCK_CV_ha_OTK_Cult(SH,SC,SK)=CV_HA_OTK_Cult(SC,SK);

         CV_HA_Cult(SC)= sum(SK,  CV_ha_OTK_Cult(SC,SK))+ (Cout_Recolte_Cult(SC,SH) + Cout_Transp_Cult(SC,SH))*Rdt_Cult(SC,SH) ;
         STOCK_CV_Ha_Cult(SH,SC)=CV_HA_Cult(SC);

*ooooooo Calcul des subventions par culture (en euros /ha/an)
         SUB_POSEI_Ha_Cult(SC) =  POSEI_Surf_Cult(SC,SH)
                                 + ((POSEI_Q_Cult(SC,SH) + Aide_Indus_Cult(SC,SH)) * Rdt_Cult(SC,SH))/Duree_Cycle_Cult(SC,SH)*12
                                 + (Aide_Replant_Cult(SC,SH) / Duree_Plant_Cult(SC,SH) ) ;
         STOCK_SUB_POSEI_Ha_Cult(SH,SC) = SUB_POSEI_Ha_Cult(SC);

         SUB_NAT_Ha_Cult(SC) = (Rdt_Cult(SC,SH) * ( Aide_Transp_Cult(SC,SH)+ Aide_Garantie_Prix_Cult(SC,SH) ))/Duree_Cycle_Cult(SC,SH)*12;
         STOCK_SUB_NAT_Ha_Cult(SH,SC)   = SUB_NAT_Ha_Cult(SC);

         SUB_PDRG_Ha_Cult(SC) = MAE_Recolte_Vert_Cult(SC,SH)+ MAE_Jachere_Sol_Nu_Cult(SC,SH)+ MAE_Compost_Cult(SC,SH);
         STOCK_SUB_PDRG_Ha_Cult(SH,SC)  = SUB_PDRG_Ha_Cult(SC);

         SUB_TOT_Ha_Cult(SC) = SUB_POSEI_Ha_Cult(SC) + SUB_NAT_Ha_Cult(SC) +   SUB_PDRG_Ha_Cult(SC) + MB_ADD_Cult(SC,SH) ;
         STOCK_SUB_TOT_Ha_Cult(SH,SC)   = SUB_TOT_Ha_Cult(SC);

*ooooooo Calcul du produit brut avec primes par culture (en euros /ha/an) oooooo
         PB_HA_Cult(SC)= ( Rdt_Cult(SC,SH) *(Prix_Cult(SC,SH)  + Bagasse_Cult(SC,SH) )+  SUB_TOT_HA_Cult(SC) )  / Duree_Cycle_Cult(SC,SH)*12 ;
         STOCK_PB_Ha_Cult(SH,SC)=PB_Ha_Cult(SC);

*ooooooo Calcul de la marge brute avec primes par culture (en euros /ha/an) oooooo
         MB_HA_Cult(SC)=  PB_HA_Cult(SC) - CV_HA_Cult(SC);
         STOCK_MB_HA_Cult(SH,SC)=MB_HA_Cult(SC);



*ooooooo Calcul du rendement inférieur espéré par culture (en t/ha/cycle)
*         RDT_Inf_Cult(SC)= Rdt_Cult(SC,SH)*Var_Rdt_Cult(SC,SH);
*         STOCK_RDT_Inf_Cult(SH,SC)=RDT_Inf_Cult(SC);

*ooooooo Calcul du produit brut inférieur avec primes par culture (en euros /ha/an) oooooo
*         CV_Inf_Ha_OTK_Cult(SC,SK)=
*                           (  Matrice_OTK_Cult(SK, SC)$(Data_OTK(SK,"AMORTI")=0)*Data_OTK(SK,"DOSE")*Data_OTK(SK,"PRIX_UNIT")
*                            +(Matrice_OTK_Cult(SK, SC)$(Data_OTK(SK,"AMORTI")=1)*Data_OTK(SK,"DOSE")*Data_OTK(SK,"PRIX_UNIT") )/Duree_Plant_Cult(SC,SH)
*                           )/ Duree_Cycle_Cult(SC,SH)*12;
*         STOCK_CV_Inf_Ha_OTK_Cult(SH,SC,SK)=CV_Inf_Ha_OTK_Cult(SC,SK);
*
*         CV_Inf_Ha_Cult(SC)= sum(SK, CV_ha_OTK_Cult(SC,SK))+((Cout_Recolte_Cult(SC,SH) + Cout_Transp_Cult(SC,SH))*Rdt_Inf_Cult(SC) );
*         STOCK_CV_Inf_Ha_Cult(SH,SC)=CV_Inf_Ha_Cult(SC);

*ooooooo Calcul du produit brut inférieur avec primes par culture (en euros /ha/an) oooooo
*         PB_Inf_Ha_Cult(SC)= (RDT_Inf_Cult(SC) *(Prix_Cult(SC,SH)  + Bagasse_Cult(SC,SH) )+  SUB_TOT_HA_Cult(SC)  )  / Duree_Cycle_Cult(SC,SH)*12;
*         STOCK_PB_Inf_Ha_Cult(SH,SC)=PB_Inf_Ha_Cult(SC);
*
*ooooooo Calcul de la marge brute avec primes par culture (en euros /ha/an) oooo
*         MB_Inf_Ha_Cult(SC)=  PB_Inf_Ha_Cult(SC) - CV_Ha_Cult(SC);
*         STOCK_MB_Inf_Ha_Cult(SH,SC)=MB_Inf_Ha_Cult(SC);



         MB_Ha_Cult_Pond(SC) = MB_Ha_Cult(SC) - MB_Ha_Cult(SC)*Var_Rdt_Cult(SC,"INIT");
         STOCK_MB_Ha_Cult_Pond(SH,SC)= MB_Ha_Cult_Pond(SC);


         MB_Ha_Cult_Marko_ARBORICULTEUR(SC) =  MB_Ha_Cult(SC) - MB_Ha_Cult_Pond(SC)* 1.30;
         MB_Ha_Cult_Marko_BANANIER(SC)      =  MB_Ha_Cult(SC) - MB_Ha_Cult_Pond(SC)* 1.20;
         MB_Ha_Cult_Marko_CANNIER(SC)       =  MB_Ha_Cult(SC) - MB_Ha_Cult_Pond(SC)* 0.30;
         MB_Ha_Cult_Marko_CANDIVMAR(SC)     =  MB_Ha_Cult(SC) - MB_Ha_Cult_Pond(SC)* 0.50;
         MB_Ha_Cult_Marko_CANDIVNOMAR(SC)   =  MB_Ha_Cult(SC) - MB_Ha_Cult_Pond(SC)* 1.60;
         MB_Ha_Cult_Marko_DIVERSIFIE(SC)    =  MB_Ha_Cult(SC) - MB_Ha_Cult_Pond(SC)* 0.55;
         MB_Ha_Cult_Marko_ELEVEUR(SC)       =  MB_Ha_Cult(SC) - MB_Ha_Cult_Pond(SC)* 2.40;
         MB_Ha_Cult_Marko_MARAICHER(SC)     =  MB_Ha_Cult(SC) - MB_Ha_Cult_Pond(SC)* 0.00;
         MB_Ha_Cult_Marko_MIXTE(SC)         =  MB_Ha_Cult(SC) - MB_Ha_Cult_Pond(SC)* 2.30;


*ooooooo Calcul de la marge brute horaire (en euros /h) oooooooooooooooooooooooo
         MO_ha_OTK_Cult(SC,SK)=
                           (  Matrice_OTK_Cult(SK, SC)$(Data_OTK(SK,"AMORTI")=0)*Data_OTK(SK,"DOSE")*Data_OTK(SK,"MO_EXPL")
                            +(Matrice_OTK_Cult(SK, SC)$(Data_OTK(SK,"AMORTI")=1)*Data_OTK(SK,"DOSE")*Data_OTK(SK,"MO_EXPL") )/Duree_Plant_Cult(SC,SH)
                           )/ Duree_Cycle_Cult(SC,SH)*12;
         STOCK_MO_ha_OTK_Cult(SH,SC,SK)=MO_HA_OTK_Cult(SC,SK);

         MO_Ha_Cult(SC)= sum(SK, MO_ha_OTK_Cult(SC,SK));
         STOCK_MO_Ha_Cult(SH,SC)=MO_HA_Cult(SC);

         MBHORAIRE_Ha_Cult(SC)$(MO_Ha_Cult(SC))=  MB_Ha_Cult(SC) / MO_Ha_Cult(SC) ;
         STOCK_MBHORAIRE_Ha_Cult(SH,SC)= MBHORAIRE_Ha_Cult(SC);

*ooooooo Calcul des quantités d'azote apportés par OTK puis par culture (en kg de N /ha/an)
         AZOTE_ha_OTK_Cult(SC,SK)=
                           (  Matrice_OTK_Cult(SK, SC)$(Data_OTK(SK,"AMORTI")=0)*Data_OTK(SK,"DOSE")*Data_OTK(SK,"AZOTE")
                            +(Matrice_OTK_Cult(SK, SC)$(Data_OTK(SK,"AMORTI")=1)*Data_OTK(SK,"DOSE")*Data_OTK(SK,"AZOTE") )/Duree_Plant_Cult(SC,SH)
                           )/ Duree_Cycle_Cult(SC,SH)*12;
         STOCK_AZOTE_ha_OTK_Cult(SH,SC,SK)=AZOTE_ha_OTK_Cult(SC,SK);

         AZOTE_Ha_Cult(SC)= sum(SK, AZOTE_ha_OTK_Cult(SC,SK));
         STOCK_AZOTE_Ha_Cult(SH,SC)=AZOTE_Ha_Cult(SC);

*ooooooo Calcul de l'IFT par OTK puis par culture (en unité /ha/an) oooooooooooo
         IFT_ha_OTK_Cult(SC,SK)=
                           (  Matrice_OTK_Cult(SK, SC)$(Data_OTK(SK,"AMORTI")=0)*Data_OTK(SK,"IFT")
                            +(Matrice_OTK_Cult(SK, SC)$(Data_OTK(SK,"AMORTI")=1)*Data_OTK(SK,"IFT") )/Duree_Plant_Cult(SC,SH)
                           )/ Duree_Cycle_Cult(SC,SH)*12;
         STOCK_IFT_ha_OTK_Cult(SH,SC,SK)=IFT_ha_OTK_Cult(SC,SK);

         IFT_Ha_Cult(SC)= sum(SK,  IFT_ha_OTK_Cult(SC,SK));
         STOCK_IFT_Ha_Cult(SH,SC)=IFT_Ha_Cult(SC);

*ooooooo Calcul des GES emis par OTK (en t de CO2 /ha/an), par culture (en t de CO2 /ha/an), puis par kg de production (en t de CO2/kg/an)
         GES_ha_OTK_Cult(SC,SK)=
                           (( Matrice_OTK_Cult(SK, SC)$(Data_OTK(SK,"AMORTI")=0)*Data_OTK(SK,"GES_Surf")
                            + Matrice_OTK_Cult(SK, SC)$(Data_OTK(SK,"AMORTI")=0)*Data_OTK(SK,"GES_Q")*Rdt_Cult(SC,SH)
                            +(Matrice_OTK_Cult(SK, SC)$(Data_OTK(SK,"AMORTI")=1)*Data_OTK(SK,"GES_Surf") )/Duree_Plant_Cult(SC,SH)
                           )/ Duree_Cycle_Cult(SC,SH)*12) / COEFF_C_CO2;
         STOCK_GES_ha_OTK_Cult(SH,SC,SK)=GES_ha_OTK_Cult(SC,SK);

         GES_Ha_Cult(SC)= sum(SK, GES_ha_OTK_Cult(SC,SK));
         STOCK_GES_Ha_Cult(SH,SC)= GES_Ha_Cult(SC);

         GES_Kg_Cult(SC)$Rdt_Cult(SC,SH)= GES_Ha_Cult(SC)/(Rdt_Cult(SC,SH)*1000);
         STOCK_GES_Kg_Cult(SH,SC)=GES_Kg_Cult(SC);


*ooooooo Calcul des rendements par groupe de culture pour les contraintes de maximum regionaux (en t/ha/cycle)
         RDT_Cult_BA(SC_BAN_EX)=Rdt_Cult(SC_BAN_EX,SH);
         STOCK_RDT_Cult_BA(SH,SC) = RDT_Cult_BA(SC);

         RDT_Cult_ME("ME")=Rdt_Cult("ME",SH);
         STOCK_RDT_Cult_ME(SH,SC) = RDT_Cult_ME(SC);

         RDT_Cult_AN(SC_AN)=Rdt_Cult(SC_AN,SH);
         STOCK_RDT_Cult_AN(SH,SC) = RDT_Cult_AN(SC);

         RDT_Cult_CS(SC_CS)=Rdt_Cult(SC_CS,SH);

         STOCK_RDT_Cult_CS(SH,SC) = RDT_Cult_CS(SC);

         RDT_Cult_IG(SC_IG)=Rdt_Cult(SC_IG,SH);
         STOCK_RDT_Cult_IG(SH,SC) = RDT_Cult_IG(SC);

         RDT_Cult_BC(SC_BC)=Rdt_Cult(SC_BC,SH);
         STOCK_RDT_Cult_BC(SH,SC) = RDT_Cult_BC(SC);

         RDT_Cult_MA(SC_MA)=Rdt_Cult(SC_MA,SH);
         STOCK_RDT_Cult_MA(SH,SC) = RDT_Cult_MA(SC);

         RDT_Cult_PLU(SC_PLU)=Rdt_Cult(SC_PLU,SH);
         STOCK_RDT_Cult_PLU(SH,SC) = RDT_Cult_PLU(SC);




         RDT_Cult_CF(SC_CF)=Rdt_Cult(SC_CF,SH);
         STOCK_RDT_Cult_CF(SH,SC) = RDT_Cult_CF(SC);



*SSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSS
*                               SOLVE
*SSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSS

option limrow=0;
option limcol=0;

if(NB_BOUCLE=0,
                         SOLVE  INIT using mip maximizing REV;
);

if(NB_BOUCLE=1,
                         SOLVE  CALIB using mip maximizing REV_MARKOVITZ_GWAD;
);

if(NB_BOUCLE>1,
                         SOLVE  SCENARIO using mip maximizing REV_MARKOVITZ_GWAD;

);


*SSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSS
*                             FIN SOLVE
*SSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSSS

MATRICE_TYPE_EXPL(ST,SE)=0;

*ooooooo Calcul des assolements aux différentes echelles (en hectares) ooooooooo

ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)      = X.l(SP,SC);


         STOCK_ASSOL_Parc(SH,SP,SC) = ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC);



LOOP((SP,SC),
         IF (NB_BOUCLE=0,
                  IF(Assol_Parc(SP,SC)>0, Matrice_Parc_Cult_Init(SP,SC) =  1);
         );
);


LOOP((SP,SC),
         IF (NB_BOUCLE=1,
                  IF(Assol_Parc(SP,SC)>0, Matrice_Parc_Cult_Calib(SP,SC) =  1);
         );
);

LOOP((SP,SC)$Matrice_Parc_Cult_Init(SP,SC),
                  IF(Matrice_Parc_Cult_Init(SP,SC) = Matrice_Parc_Cult_Calib(SP,SC), Matrice_Parc_Cult_PAD(SP,SC)=1
                  );
);

Matrice_Parc_PAD(SP)=SUM(SC, Matrice_Parc_Cult_PAD(SP,SC) );


NB_Parc_Init=CARD(SP);


PAD_Parc= (NB_Parc_Init - SUM(SP, Matrice_Parc_PAD(SP)) )/ NB_Parc_Init;


EFF_PARC_CULT_INIT(SC)=SUM(SP$Matrice_Parc_Cult_Init(SP,SC),Matrice_Parc_Cult_Init(SP, SC));
EFF_PARC_CULT_CALIB(SC)=SUM(SP$Matrice_Parc_Cult_Init(SP,SC),Matrice_Parc_Cult_CALIB(SP, SC));
PAD_PARC_CULT(SC)$EFF_PARC_CULT_INIT(SC)= ABS( (EFF_PARC_CULT_INIT(SC) - EFF_PARC_CULT_CALIB(SC)) / EFF_PARC_CULT_INIT(SC));








         ASSOL_Expl(SE,SC)          = sum(SP$Expl_Parc(SE,SP),    ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC) );
         STOCK_ASSOL_Expl(SH,SE,SC) = ASSOL_Expl(SE,SC);

         ASSOL_Reg(SR,SC)           = sum(SP$Reg_Parc(SR,SP),     ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC) );
         STOCK_ASSOL_Reg(SH,SR,SC)  = ASSOL_Reg(SR,SC);

         ASSOL_Gwad(SC)             = sum(SP,                     ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC) );
         STOCK_ASSOL_Gwad(SH,SC)    = ASSOL_Gwad(SC);

         ASSOL_BV(BV,SC)            = sum(SP$BV_Parc(BV,SP),      ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC) );
         STOCK_ASSOL_BV(SH,BV,SC)   = ASSOL_BV(BV,SC);

*         ASSOL_RIV(RIV,SC)          = sum(SP$RIV_Parc(RIV,SP),    ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC) );
*         STOCK_ASSOL_RIV(SH,RIV,SC) = ASSOL_RIV(RIV,SC);

         ASSOL_CPT(CPT,SC)          = sum(SP$CPT_Parc(CPT,SP),    ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC) );
         STOCK_ASSOL_CPT(SH,CPT,SC) = ASSOL_CPT(CPT,SC);


         ASSOL_SOBT(SRSOBT,SC)         = sum(SP$Reg_Parc(SRSOBT,SP), ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC) );
         STOCK_ASSOL_SOBT(SH,SRSOBT,SC)= ASSOL_SOBT(SRSOBT,SC);

         ASSOL_SEBT(SRSEBT,SC)         = sum(SP$Reg_Parc(SRSEBT,SP), ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC) );
         STOCK_ASSOL_SEBT(SH,SRSEBT,SC)= ASSOL_SEBT(SRSEBT,SC);

         ASSOL_NBT(SRNBT,SC)           = sum(SP$Reg_Parc(SRNBT,SP),  ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC) );
         STOCK_ASSOL_NBT(SH,SRNBT,SC)  = ASSOL_NBT(SRNBT,SC);

         ASSOL_NGT(SRNGT,SC)           = sum(SP$Reg_Parc(SRNGT,SP),  ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC) );
         STOCK_ASSOL_NGT(SH,SRNGT,SC)  = ASSOL_NGT(SRNGT,SC);

         ASSOL_CGT(SRCGT,SC)           = sum(SP$Reg_Parc(SRCGT,SP),  ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC) );
         STOCK_ASSOL_CGT(SH,SRCGT,SC)  = ASSOL_CGT(SRCGT,SC);

         ASSOL_EGT(SREGT,SC)           = sum(SP$Reg_Parc(SREGT,SP),  ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC) );
         STOCK_ASSOL_EGT(SH,SREGT,SC)  = ASSOL_EGT(SREGT,SC);

         ASSOL_MG(SRMG,SC)             = sum(SP$Reg_Parc(SRMG,SP),   ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC) );
         STOCK_ASSOL_MG(SH,SRMG,SC)    = ASSOL_MG(SRMG,SC);


         ASSOL_SOBT_tot(SC)          = sum (SRSOBT,  ASSOL_SOBT(SRSOBT,SC) );
         STOCK_ASSOL_SOBT_tot(SH,SC) = ASSOL_SOBT_tot(SC);

         ASSOL_SEBT_tot(SC)          = sum (SRSEBT,  ASSOL_SEBT(SRSEBT,SC) );
         STOCK_ASSOL_SEBT_tot(SH,SC) = ASSOL_SEBT_tot(SC);

         ASSOL_NBT_tot(SC)           = sum (SRNBT,   ASSOL_NBT(SRNBT,SC)   );
         STOCK_ASSOL_NBT_tot(SH,SC)  = ASSOL_NBT_tot(SC);

         ASSOL_EGT_tot(SC)           = sum (SREGT,   ASSOL_EGT(SREGT,SC)   );
         STOCK_ASSOL_EGT_tot(SH,SC)  = ASSOL_EGT_tot(SC);

         ASSOL_CGT_tot(SC)           = sum (SRCGT,   ASSOL_CGT(SRCGT,SC)   );
         STOCK_ASSOL_CGT_tot(SH,SC)  = ASSOL_CGT_tot(SC);

         ASSOL_NGT_tot(SC)           = sum (SRNGT,   ASSOL_NGT(SRNGT,SC)   );
         STOCK_ASSOL_NGT_tot(SH,SC)  = ASSOL_NGT_tot(SC);

         ASSOL_MG_tot(SC)            = sum (SRMG,    ASSOL_MG(SRMG,SC)     );
         STOCK_ASSOL_MG_tot(SH,SC)   = ASSOL_MG_tot(SC);

         ASSOL_GWAD_tot(SC)          = ASSOL_GWAD(SC)                       ;
         STOCK_ASSOL_GWAD_tot(SH,SC) = ASSOL_GWAD_tot(SC);



         ASSOL_SOBT_ANA(SRSOBT,SC_AN) = sum(SP$Reg_Parc(SRSOBT,SP), X.l(SP,SC_AN ));
         STOCK_ASSOL_SOBT_ANA(SH,SRSOBT,SC_AN) = ASSOL_SOBT_ANA(SRSOBT,SC_AN);

         ASSOL_SEBT_ANA(SRSEBT,SC_AN) = sum(SP$Reg_Parc(SRSEBT,SP), X.l(SP,SC_AN ));
         STOCK_ASSOL_SEBT_ANA(SH,SRSEBT,SC_AN) = ASSOL_SEBT_ANA(SRSEBT,SC_AN);

         ASSOL_NBT_ANA(SRNBT,SC_AN)   = sum(SP$Reg_Parc(SRNBT,SP), X.l(SP,SC_AN ));
         STOCK_ASSOL_NBT_ANA(SH,SRNBT,SC_AN)   = ASSOL_NBT_ANA(SRNBT,SC_AN);

         ASSOL_NGT_ANA(SRNGT,SC_AN)   = sum(SP$Reg_Parc(SRNGT,SP), X.l(SP,SC_AN ));
         STOCK_ASSOL_NGT_ANA(SH,SRNGT,SC_AN)   = ASSOL_NGT_ANA(SRNGT,SC_AN);

         ASSOL_CGT_ANA(SRCGT,SC_AN)   = sum(SP$Reg_Parc(SRCGT,SP), X.l(SP,SC_AN ));
         STOCK_ASSOL_CGT_ANA(SH,SRCGT,SC_AN)   = ASSOL_CGT_ANA(SRCGT,SC_AN);

         ASSOL_EGT_ANA(SREGT,SC_AN)   = sum(SP$Reg_Parc(SREGT,SP), X.l(SP,SC_AN ));
         STOCK_ASSOL_EGT_ANA(SH,SREGT,SC_AN)   = ASSOL_EGT_ANA(SREGT,SC_AN);

         ASSOL_MG_ANA(SRMG,SC_AN)     = sum(SP$Reg_Parc(SRMG,SP), X.l(SP,SC_AN ));
         STOCK_ASSOL_MG_ANA(SH,SRMG,SC_AN)     = ASSOL_MG_ANA(SRMG,SC_AN);

         ASSOL_GWAD_ANA(SR,SC_AN)     = sum(SP$Reg_Parc(SR,SP), X.l(SP,SC_AN ));
         STOCK_ASSOL_GWAD_ANA(SH,SR,SC_AN)     = ASSOL_GWAD_ANA(SR,SC_AN);


         ASSOL_SOBT_BAN(SRSOBT,SC_BAN) = sum(SP$Reg_Parc(SRSOBT,SP), X.l(SP,SC_BAN));
         STOCK_ASSOL_SOBT_BAN(SH,SRSOBT,SC_BAN) = ASSOL_SOBT_BAN(SRSOBT,SC_BAN);

         ASSOL_SEBT_BAN(SRSEBT,SC_BAN) = sum(SP$Reg_Parc(SRSEBT,SP), X.l(SP,SC_BAN));
         STOCK_ASSOL_SEBT_BAN(SH,SRSEBT,SC_BAN) = ASSOL_SEBT_BAN(SRSEBT,SC_BAN);

         ASSOL_NBT_BAN(SRNBT,SC_BAN)   = sum(SP$Reg_Parc(SRNBT,SP), X.l(SP,SC_BAN));
         STOCK_ASSOL_NBT_BAN(SH,SRNBT,SC_BAN)   = ASSOL_NBT_BAN(SRNBT,SC_BAN);

         ASSOL_NGT_BAN(SRNGT,SC_BAN)   = sum(SP$Reg_Parc(SRNGT,SP), X.l(SP,SC_BAN));
         STOCK_ASSOL_NGT_BAN(SH,SRNGT,SC_BAN)   = ASSOL_NGT_BAN(SRNGT,SC_BAN);

         ASSOL_CGT_BAN(SRCGT,SC_BAN)   = sum(SP$Reg_Parc(SRCGT,SP), X.l(SP,SC_BAN));
         STOCK_ASSOL_CGT_BAN(SH,SRCGT,SC_BAN)   = ASSOL_CGT_BAN(SRCGT,SC_BAN);

         ASSOL_EGT_BAN(SREGT,SC_BAN)   = sum(SP$Reg_Parc(SREGT,SP), X.l(SP,SC_BAN));
         STOCK_ASSOL_EGT_BAN(SH,SREGT,SC_BAN)   = ASSOL_EGT_BAN(SREGT,SC_BAN) ;

         ASSOL_MG_BAN(SRMG,SC_BAN)     = sum(SP$Reg_Parc(SRMG,SP), X.l(SP,SC_BAN));
         STOCK_ASSOL_MG_BAN(SH,SRMG,SC_BAN)     = ASSOL_MG_BAN(SRMG,SC_BAN) ;

         ASSOL_GWAD_BAN(SR,SC_BAN)     = sum(SP$Reg_Parc(SR,SP), X.l(SP,SC_BAN));
         STOCK_ASSOL_GWAD_BAN(SH,SR,SC_BAN)     = ASSOL_GWAD_BAN(SR,SC_BAN) ;


         ASSOL_SOBT_BAN_EX(SRSOBT,SC_BAN_EX) = sum(SP$Reg_Parc(SRSOBT,SP), X.l(SP,SC_BAN_EX));
         STOCK_ASSOL_SOBT_BAN_EX(SH,SRSOBT,SC_BAN_EX) = ASSOL_SOBT_BAN_EX(SRSOBT,SC_BAN_EX);

         STOCK_ASSOL_EGT_tot_NON(SH)  = ASSOL_EGT_tot_NON;

         ASSOL_MG_tot_NON = sum(SRMG, sum(SC_NON, ASSOL_MG_NON(SRMG, SC_NON)));
         STOCK_ASSOL_MG_tot_NON(SH)   = ASSOL_MG_tot_NON;

         ASSOL_GWAD_tot_NON = sum(SR, sum(SC_NON, ASSOL_GWAD_NON(SR, SC_NON)));
         STOCK_ASSOL_GWAD_tot_NON(SH)   = ASSOL_GWAD_tot_NON;


         ASSOL_SOBT_tot_FRICHES = sum(SRSOBT, sum(SC_FRICHES, ASSOL_SOBT_FRICHES(SRSOBT,SC_FRICHES)));
         STOCK_ASSOL_SOBT_tot_FRICHES(SH) = ASSOL_SOBT_tot_FRICHES;

         STOCK_ASSOL_SEBT_tot_FRICHES(SH) = ASSOL_SEBT_tot_FRICHES;

         ASSOL_NBT_tot_FRICHES = sum(SRNBT, sum(SC_FRICHES, ASSOL_NBT_FRICHES(SRNBT,SC_FRICHES)));
         STOCK_ASSOL_NBT_tot_FRICHES(SH)  = ASSOL_NBT_tot_FRICHES;

         ASSOL_NGT_tot_FRICHES = sum(SRNGT, sum(SC_FRICHES, ASSOL_NGT_FRICHES(SRNGT,SC_FRICHES)));
         STOCK_ASSOL_NGT_tot_FRICHES(SH)  = ASSOL_NGT_tot_FRICHES;

         ASSOL_CGT_tot_FRICHES = sum(SRCGT, sum(SC_FRICHES, ASSOL_CGT_FRICHES(SRCGT,SC_FRICHES)));
         STOCK_ASSOL_CGT_tot_FRICHES(SH)  = ASSOL_CGT_tot_FRICHES;

         ASSOL_EGT_tot_FRICHES = sum(SREGT, sum(SC_FRICHES, ASSOL_EGT_FRICHES(SREGT,SC_FRICHES)));

         ASSOL_MG_tot_FRICHES = sum(SRMG, sum(SC_FRICHES, ASSOL_MG_FRICHES(SRMG, SC_FRICHES)));
         STOCK_ASSOL_MG_tot_FRICHES(SH)   = ASSOL_MG_tot_FRICHES;

         ASSOL_GWAD_tot_FRICHES = sum(SR, sum(SC_FRICHES, ASSOL_GWAD_FRICHES(SR, SC_FRICHES)));
         STOCK_ASSOL_GWAD_tot_FRICHES(SH)   = ASSOL_GWAD_tot_FRICHES;


         ASSOL_SOBT_tot_JA = sum(SRSOBT, sum(SC_JA, ASSOL_SOBT_JA(SRSOBT,SC_JA)));

         ASSOL_GWAD_tot_JA = sum(SR, sum(SC_JA, ASSOL_GWAD_JA(SR, SC_JA)));
         STOCK_ASSOL_GWAD_tot_JA(SH)   = ASSOL_GWAD_tot_JA;


         ASSOL_SOBT_tot_PAT = sum(SRSOBT, sum(SC_PAT, ASSOL_SOBT_PAT(SRSOBT,SC_PAT)));
         STOCK_ASSOL_SOBT_tot_PAT(SH) = ASSOL_SOBT_tot_PAT;

         ASSOL_SEBT_tot_PAT = sum(SRSEBT, sum(SC_PAT, ASSOL_SEBT_PAT(SRSEBT,SC_PAT)));
         STOCK_ASSOL_SEBT_tot_PAT(SH) = ASSOL_SEBT_tot_PAT;

         ASSOL_NBT_tot_PAT = sum(SRNBT, sum(SC_PAT, ASSOL_NBT_PAT(SRNBT,SC_PAT)));
         STOCK_ASSOL_NBT_tot_PAT(SH)  = ASSOL_NBT_tot_PAT;

         ASSOL_NGT_tot_PAT = sum(SRNGT, sum(SC_PAT, ASSOL_NGT_PAT(SRNGT,SC_PAT)));
         STOCK_ASSOL_NGT_tot_PAT(SH)  = ASSOL_NGT_tot_PAT;

         ASSOL_CGT_tot_PAT = sum(SRCGT, sum(SC_PAT, ASSOL_CGT_PAT(SRCGT,SC_PAT)));
         STOCK_ASSOL_CGT_tot_PAT(SH)  = ASSOL_CGT_tot_PAT;

         ASSOL_EGT_tot_PAT = sum(SREGT, sum(SC_PAT, ASSOL_EGT_PAT(SREGT,SC_PAT)));

         ASSOL_SOBT_tot_PLU = sum(SRSOBT, sum(SC_PLU, ASSOL_SOBT_PLU(SRSOBT,SC_PLU)));

         ASSOL_SOBT_tot_TOTAL = sum(SRSOBT, sum(SC_TOT, ASSOL_SOBT_TOTAL(SRSOBT,SC_TOT)));

         STOCK_ASSOL_EGT_tot_TOTAL(SH)  = ASSOL_EGT_tot_TOTAL;

         STOCK_ASSOL_MG_tot_TOTAL(SH)   = ASSOL_MG_tot_TOTAL;

         ASSOL_GWAD_tot_TOTAL = sum(SR, sum(SC_TOT, ASSOL_GWAD_TOTAL(SR, SC_TOT)));
         STOCK_ASSOL_GWAD_tot_TOTAL(SH)   = ASSOL_GWAD_tot_TOTAL;


*ooooooo Calcul des valeurs de surface aux différentes echelles (en ha) oooooooo

         SURF_Parc(SP)           = sum(SC,                     ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC) );
         STOCK_SURF_Parc(SH,SP)  = SURF_Parc(SP);

         SURF_Cult(SC)           = sum(SP,                     ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC) );
         STOCK_SURF_Cult(SH,SC)  = SURF_Cult(SC);

         SURF_EXPL(SE)           = sum(SP$Expl_Parc(SE,SP),    SURF_Parc(SP) );
         STOCK_SURF_EXPL(SH,SE)  = SURF_Expl(SE);

         SURF_Reg(SR)            = sum(SP$Reg_Parc(SR,SP),     SURF_Parc(SP) );
         STOCK_SURF_Reg(SH,SR)   = SURF_Reg(SR);

         SURF_Gwad               = sum(SP,                     SURF_Parc(SP) );
         STOCK_SURF_Gwad(SH)     = SURF_Gwad;

         SURF_BV(SBV)            = sum(SP$BV_Parc(SBV,SP),     SURF_Parc(SP) );
         STOCK_SURF_BV(SH,SBV)   = SURF_BV(SBV);

*         SURF_RIV(SRIV)          = sum(SP$RIV_Parc(SRIV,SP),   SURF_Parc(SP) );
*         STOCK_SURF_RIV(SH,SRIV) = SURF_RIV(SRIV);

         SURF_CPT(SCPT)          = sum(SP$CPT_Parc(SCPT,SP),   SURF_Parc(SP) );
         STOCK_SURF_CPT(SH,SCPT) = SURF_CPT(SCPT);


         SURF_SOBT(SRSOBT)          = sum(SP$Reg_Parc(SRSOBT,SP), SURF_Parc(SP) );
         STOCK_SURF_SOBT(SH,SRSOBT) = SURF_SOBT(SRSOBT);

         SURF_SEBT(SRSEBT)          = sum(SP$Reg_Parc(SRSEBT,SP), SURF_Parc(SP) );
         STOCK_SURF_SEBT(SH,SRSEBT) = SURF_SEBT(SRSEBT);

         SURF_NBT(SRNBT)            = sum(SP$Reg_Parc(SRNBT,SP),  SURF_Parc(SP) );
         STOCK_SURF_NBT(SH,SRNBT)   = SURF_NBT(SRNBT);

         SURF_EGT(SREGT)            = sum(SP$Reg_Parc(SREGT,SP),  SURF_Parc(SP) );
         STOCK_SURF_EGT(SH,SREGT)   = SURF_EGT(SREGT);

         SURF_CGT(SRCGT)            = sum(SP$Reg_Parc(SRCGT,SP),  SURF_Parc(SP) );
         STOCK_SURF_CGT(SH,SRCGT)   = SURF_CGT(SRCGT);

         SURF_NGT(SRNGT)            = sum(SP$Reg_Parc(SRNGT,SP),  SURF_Parc(SP) );
         STOCK_SURF_NGT(SH,SRNGT)   = SURF_NGT(SRNGT);

         SURF_MG(SRMG)              = sum(SP$Reg_Parc(SRMG,SP),   SURF_Parc(SP) );
         STOCK_SURF_MG(SH,SRMG)     = SURF_MG(SRMG);


         SURF_SOBT_tot           = sum (SRSOBT, SURF_SOBT(SRSOBT) );
         STOCK_SURF_SOBT_tot(SH) = SURF_SOBT_tot;

         SURF_SEBT_tot           = sum (SRSEBT, SURF_SEBT(SRSEBT) );
         STOCK_SURF_SEBT_tot(SH) = SURF_SEBT_tot;

         SURF_NBT_tot            = sum (SRNBT,  SURF_NBT(SRNBT)   );
         STOCK_SURF_NBT_tot(SH)  = SURF_NBT_tot;

         SURF_EGT_tot            = sum (SREGT,  SURF_EGT(SREGT)   );
         STOCK_SURF_EGT_tot(SH)  = SURF_EGT_tot;

         SURF_CGT_tot            = sum (SRCGT,  SURF_CGT(SRCGT)   );
         STOCK_SURF_CGT_tot(SH)  = SURF_CGT_tot;

         SURF_NGT_tot            = sum (SRNGT,  SURF_NGT(SRNGT)   );
         STOCK_SURF_NGT_tot(SH)  = SURF_NGT_tot;

         SURF_MG_tot             = sum (SRMG,   SURF_MG(SRMG)     );
         STOCK_SURF_MG_tot(SH)   = SURF_MG_tot;



****************************************************************************************************************************************************************
******* Calcul liés aux types d'exploitations ******************************************************************************************************************
****************************************************************************************************************************************************************

         SURF_CUL(SE)=sum(SC_CULTIV, ASSOL_Expl(SE,SC_CULTIV));
         STOCK_SURF_CUL(SH,SE) = SURF_CUL(SE);

         SURF_CAN(SE)=sum(SC_CAN,    ASSOL_Expl(SE,SC_CAN));
         STOCK_SURF_CAN(SH,SE) = SURF_CAN(SE);

         SURF_CF(SE)=sum(SC_CF,    ASSOL_Expl(SE,SC_CF));
         STOCK_SURF_CF(SH,SE) = SURF_CF(SE);

         SURF_BAN(SE)=sum(SC_BAN_EX, ASSOL_Expl(SE,SC_BAN_EX));
         STOCK_SURF_BAN(SH,SE) = SURF_BAN(SE);

         SURF_PAT(SE)=sum(SC_PAT,    ASSOL_Expl(SE,SC_PAT));
         STOCK_SURF_PAT(SH,SE) = SURF_PAT(SE);

         SURF_NON(SE)=sum(SC_NON,    ASSOL_Expl(SE,SC_NON));
         STOCK_SURF_NON(SH,SE) = SURF_NON(SE);

         SURF_ANA(SE)=sum(SC_AN,     ASSOL_Expl(SE,SC_AN));
         STOCK_SURF_ANA(SH,SE) = SURF_ANA(SE);

         SURF_PLU(SE)=sum(SC_PLU,    ASSOL_Expl(SE,SC_PLU));
         STOCK_SURF_PLU(SH,SE) = SURF_PLU(SE);

         SURF_MAR(SE)=sum(SC_MAR,    ASSOL_Expl(SE,SC_MAR));
         STOCK_SURF_MAR(SH,SE) = SURF_MAR(SE);

         SURF_BC(SE) =sum(SC_BC,     ASSOL_Expl(SE,SC_BC));
         STOCK_SURF_BC(SH,SE)  = SURF_BC(SE);

         SURF_TT(SE)= sum(SC_IG,     ASSOL_Expl(SE,SC_IG));
         STOCK_SURF_TT(SH,SE)  = SURF_TT(SE);


         PART_CAN(SE)$(SURF_CUL(SE) - SURF_NON(SE)) = SURF_CAN(SE) / (SURF_CUL(SE) - SURF_NON(SE));
         STOCK_PART_CAN(SH,SE) = PART_CAN(SE);

         PART_BAN(SE)$(SURF_CUL(SE) - SURF_NON(SE)) = SURF_BAN(SE) / (SURF_CUL(SE) - SURF_NON(SE));
         STOCK_PART_BAN(SH,SE) = PART_BAN(SE);

         PART_PAT(SE)$(SURF_CUL(SE) - SURF_NON(SE)) = SURF_PAT(SE) / (SURF_CUL(SE) - SURF_NON(SE));
         STOCK_PART_PAT(SH,SE) = PART_PAT(SE);

         PART_NON(SE)$(SURF_CUL(SE) - SURF_NON(SE)) = SURF_NON(SE) / (SURF_CUL(SE) - SURF_NON(SE));
         STOCK_PART_NON(SH,SE) = PART_NON(SE);

         PART_ANA(SE)$(SURF_CUL(SE) - SURF_NON(SE)) = SURF_ANA(SE) / (SURF_CUL(SE) - SURF_NON(SE));
         STOCK_PART_ANA(SH,SE) = PART_ANA(SE);

         PART_PLU(SE)$(SURF_CUL(SE) - SURF_NON(SE)) = SURF_PLU(SE) / (SURF_CUL(SE) - SURF_NON(SE));
         STOCK_PART_PLU(SH,SE) = PART_PLU(SE);

         PART_MAR(SE)$(SURF_CUL(SE) - SURF_NON(SE)) = SURF_MAR(SE) / (SURF_CUL(SE) - SURF_NON(SE));
         STOCK_PART_MAR(SH,SE) = PART_MAR(SE);

         PART_BC(SE) $(SURF_CUL(SE) - SURF_NON(SE)) = SURF_BC(SE)  / (SURF_CUL(SE) - SURF_NON(SE));
         STOCK_PART_BC(SH,SE)  = PART_BC(SE);

         PART_TT(SE) $(SURF_CUL(SE) - SURF_NON(SE)) = SURF_TT(SE)  / (SURF_CUL(SE) - SURF_NON(SE));
         STOCK_PART_TT(SH,SE)  = PART_TT(SE);


*Types d'exploitation: 1=Arboriculteurs, 2=Bananiers, 3=Canniers, 4=Canniers diversifiés, 5=Diversifiés, 6=Eleveurs, 7=Maraîchers, 8=Mixtes canniers-éleveurs, 0=Frichiers.
         LOOP(SE,
                  IF ( PART_PAT(SE)>= 0.33 and PART_CAN(SE) <  0.37,  TYPE_EXPL(SE)=6; );
                  IF ( PART_PAT(SE)>= 0.33 and PART_CAN(SE) >= 0.37,  TYPE_EXPL(SE)=8; );
                  IF ( PART_PAT(SE)<  0.33 and PART_CAN(SE) >= 0.68,  TYPE_EXPL(SE)=3; );
                  IF ( PART_PAT(SE)<  0.33 and PART_CAN(SE) <  0.68 and PART_CAN(SE) >= 0.33, TYPE_EXPL(SE)=4; );
                  IF ( PART_PAT(SE)<  0.33 and PART_CAN(SE) <  0.68 and PART_CAN(SE) < 0.33 and PART_BAN(SE) >= 0.4, TYPE_EXPL(SE)=2; );
                  IF ( PART_PAT(SE)<  0.33 and PART_CAN(SE) <  0.68 and PART_CAN(SE) < 0.33 and PART_BAN(SE) < 0.4 and PART_MAR(SE) >= 0.64, TYPE_EXPL(SE)=7; );
                  IF ( PART_PAT(SE)<  0.33 and PART_CAN(SE) <  0.68 and PART_CAN(SE) < 0.33 and PART_BAN(SE) < 0.4 and PART_MAR(SE) < 0.64 and PART_PLU(SE) >= 0.55, TYPE_EXPL(SE)=1; );
                  IF ( PART_PAT(SE)<  0.33 and PART_CAN(SE) <  0.68 and PART_CAN(SE) < 0.33 and PART_BAN(SE) < 0.4 and PART_MAR(SE) < 0.64 and PART_PLU(SE) < 0.55 and SURF_CUL(SE)>0, TYPE_EXPL(SE)=5; );
                  IF ( SURF_CUL(SE)=0, TYPE_EXPL(SE)=10;);
         );

         LOOP(SE,
                  IF ( PART_CAN(SE)>= 0.625 and PART_CAN(SE) <  0.939, TYPE_EXPL(SE)=4; );
                  IF ( PART_CAN(SE)>= 0.939,TYPE_EXPL(SE)=3; );
                  IF ( PART_CAN(SE)<  0.625 and PART_PAT(SE) >= 0.606, TYPE_EXPL(SE)=6; );
                  IF ( PART_CAN(SE)<  0.625 and PART_PAT(SE) <  0.606 and PART_PAT(SE) >= 0.327, TYPE_EXPL(SE)=8; );
                  IF ( PART_CAN(SE)<  0.625 and PART_PAT(SE) <  0.606 and PART_PAT(SE) < 0.327 and PART_BAN(SE) >= 0.364, TYPE_EXPL(SE)=2; );
                  IF ( PART_CAN(SE)<  0.625 and PART_PAT(SE) <  0.606 and PART_PAT(SE) < 0.327 and PART_BAN(SE) < 0.364 and PART_MAR(SE)>= 0.801, TYPE_EXPL(SE)= 7; );
                  IF ( PART_CAN(SE)<  0.625 and PART_PAT(SE) <  0.606 and PART_PAT(SE) < 0.327 and PART_BAN(SE) < 0.364 and PART_MAR(SE)< 0.801 and PART_PLU(SE) >= 0.522, TYPE_EXPL(SE)= 1; );
                  IF ( PART_CAN(SE)<  0.625 and PART_PAT(SE) <  0.606 and PART_PAT(SE) < 0.327 and PART_BAN(SE) < 0.364 and PART_MAR(SE)< 0.801 and PART_PLU(SE) < 0.522,  TYPE_EXPL(SE)= 5; );
                  IF ( SURF_CUL(SE)=0, TYPE_EXPL(SE)=0;);
         );
         STOCK_TYPE_EXPL(SH,SE) = TYPE_EXPL(SE);


*Le type cannier diversifiés a été scindé en 2 dans la publication en fonction de la présence de culture de diversification c'est à dire maraichage, arboriculture, tubercules ou plantain
         LOOP(SE,
                 IF ( TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 4 AND ( PART_MAR(SE)>0 OR PART_PLU(SE)>0 OR PART_BC(SE)>0 OR PART_TT(SE) >0  ), TYPE_EXPL_Bis(SE)=41;);
                 IF ( TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 4 AND ( PART_MAR(SE)=0 OR PART_PLU(SE)=0 OR PART_BC(SE)=0 OR PART_TT(SE) =0  ), TYPE_EXPL_Bis(SE)=42;);
         );
         STOCK_TYPE_EXPL_Bis(SH,SE) = TYPE_EXPL_Bis(SE);






*Types d'exploitation: 1=Arboriculteurs, 2=Bananiers, 3=Canniers, 4=Canniers diversifiés
*                      5=Diversifiés, 6=Eleveurs, 7=Maraîchers, 8=Mixtes canniers-éleveurs, 0=Frichiers.



         LOOP(SE,
                 IF ( TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 0, MATRICE_TYPE_EXPL("T0",SE)=1);
                 IF ( TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 1, MATRICE_TYPE_EXPL("T1",SE)=1);
                 IF ( TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 2, MATRICE_TYPE_EXPL("T2",SE)=1);
                 IF ( TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 3, MATRICE_TYPE_EXPL("T3",SE)=1);
                 IF ( TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 4, MATRICE_TYPE_EXPL("T4",SE)=1);
                 IF ( TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 5, MATRICE_TYPE_EXPL("T5",SE)=1);
                 IF ( TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 6, MATRICE_TYPE_EXPL("T6",SE)=1);
                 IF ( TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 7, MATRICE_TYPE_EXPL("T7",SE)=1);
                 IF ( TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 8, MATRICE_TYPE_EXPL("T8",SE)=1);
         );
         STOCK_MATRICE_TYPE_EXPL(SH, ST, SE)  = MATRICE_TYPE_EXPL(ST, SE) ;



         EFF_TYPE_1_Gwad=sum(SE$(TYPE_EXPL(SE)=1), TYPE_EXPL(SE)/1);
         STOCK_EFF_TYPE_1_Gwad(SH) = EFF_TYPE_1_Gwad;

         EFF_TYPE_2_Gwad=sum(SE$(TYPE_EXPL(SE)=2), TYPE_EXPL(SE)/2);
         STOCK_EFF_TYPE_2_Gwad(SH) = EFF_TYPE_2_Gwad;

         EFF_TYPE_3_Gwad=sum(SE$(TYPE_EXPL(SE)=3), TYPE_EXPL(SE)/3);
         STOCK_EFF_TYPE_3_Gwad(SH) = EFF_TYPE_3_Gwad;

         EFF_TYPE_4_Gwad=sum(SE$(TYPE_EXPL(SE)=4), TYPE_EXPL(SE)/4);
         STOCK_EFF_TYPE_4_Gwad(SH) = EFF_TYPE_4_Gwad;

         EFF_TYPE_5_Gwad=sum(SE$(TYPE_EXPL(SE)=5), TYPE_EXPL(SE)/5);
         STOCK_EFF_TYPE_5_Gwad(SH) = EFF_TYPE_5_Gwad;

         EFF_TYPE_6_Gwad=sum(SE$(TYPE_EXPL(SE)=6), TYPE_EXPL(SE)/6);
         STOCK_EFF_TYPE_6_Gwad(SH) = EFF_TYPE_6_Gwad;

         EFF_TYPE_7_Gwad=sum(SE$(TYPE_EXPL(SE)=7), TYPE_EXPL(SE)/7);
         STOCK_EFF_TYPE_7_Gwad(SH) = EFF_TYPE_7_Gwad;

         EFF_TYPE_8_Gwad=sum(SE$(TYPE_EXPL(SE)=8), TYPE_EXPL(SE)/8);
         STOCK_EFF_TYPE_8_Gwad(SH) = EFF_TYPE_8_Gwad;

         EFF_TYPE_0_Gwad= NBRE_EXPL_init - (EFF_TYPE_1_Gwad + EFF_TYPE_2_Gwad + EFF_TYPE_3_Gwad + EFF_TYPE_4_Gwad + EFF_TYPE_5_Gwad + EFF_TYPE_6_Gwad + EFF_TYPE_7_Gwad + EFF_TYPE_8_Gwad);
         STOCK_EFF_TYPE_0_Gwad(SH) = EFF_TYPE_0_Gwad;


         PART_TYPE_1_Gwad = EFF_TYPE_1_Gwad / NBRE_EXPL_init *100;
         PART_TYPE_2_Gwad = EFF_TYPE_2_Gwad / NBRE_EXPL_init *100;
         PART_TYPE_3_Gwad = EFF_TYPE_3_Gwad / NBRE_EXPL_init *100;
         PART_TYPE_4_Gwad = EFF_TYPE_4_Gwad / NBRE_EXPL_init *100;
         PART_TYPE_5_Gwad = EFF_TYPE_5_Gwad / NBRE_EXPL_init *100;
         PART_TYPE_6_Gwad = EFF_TYPE_6_Gwad / NBRE_EXPL_init *100;
         PART_TYPE_7_Gwad = EFF_TYPE_7_Gwad / NBRE_EXPL_init *100;
         PART_TYPE_8_Gwad = EFF_TYPE_8_Gwad / NBRE_EXPL_init *100;
         PART_TYPE_0_Gwad = EFF_TYPE_0_Gwad / NBRE_EXPL_init *100;


         ALTITUDE_EXPL(SE) = (sum(SP$Expl_Parc(SE,SP), Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"ALTITUDE")   )) / sum(SP$Expl_Parc(SE,SP), Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"SURF_HA"));
         STOCK_ALTITUDE_EXPL(SH,SE) = ALTITUDE_EXPL(SE);

         PENTE_EXPL(SE)    = (sum(SP$Expl_Parc(SE,SP), Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"PENTE")      )) / sum(SP$Expl_Parc(SE,SP), Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"SURF_HA"));
         STOCK_PENTE_EXPL(SH,SE)    = PENTE_EXPL(SE);

         PLUIE_EXPL(SE)    = (sum(SP$Expl_Parc(SE,SP), Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"PLUVIO_PARC"))) / sum(SP$Expl_Parc(SE,SP), Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"SURF_HA"));
         STOCK_PLUIE_EXPL(SH,SE)    = PLUIE_EXPL(SE);

         NB_PARC_EXPL(SE)  = (sum(SP$Expl_Parc(SE,SP), Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"NB_PARC"    ))) / sum(SP$Expl_Parc(SE,SP), Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"SURF_HA"));
         STOCK_NB_PARC_EXPL(SH,SE)  = NB_PARC_EXPL(SE);


         LOOP(SE,
                  IF (TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 1 AND ALTITUDE_EXPL(SE) >= 214.27                                 , SOUS_TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 11; );
                  IF (TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 1 AND ALTITUDE_EXPL(SE) <  214.27 AND NB_PARC_EXPL(SE)  >= 5      , SOUS_TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 12; );
                  IF (TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 1 AND ALTITUDE_EXPL(SE) <  214.27 AND NB_PARC_EXPL(SE)  <  5      , SOUS_TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 13; );
                  IF (TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 2 AND PENTE_EXPL(SE)    <  15     AND PLUIE_EXPL(SE)    >= 3029   , SOUS_TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 21; );
                  IF (TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 2 AND PENTE_EXPL(SE)    <  15     AND PLUIE_EXPL(SE)    <  3029   , SOUS_TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 22; );
*                 IF (TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 2 AND PENTE_EXPL(SE)    >= 15     AND NB_CULT_EXPL(SE)  < 3       , SOUS_TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 23; );
*                 IF (TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 2 AND PENTE_EXPL(SE)    >= 15     AND NB_CULT_EXPL(SE)  >= 3      , SOUS_TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 24; );
                  IF (TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 3 AND PENTE_EXPL(SE)    >= 6.2                                    , SOUS_TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 31; );
                  IF (TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 3 AND PENTE_EXPL(SE)    <  6.2    AND SURF_Expl(SE)     <  5.91   , SOUS_TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 32; );
                  IF (TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 3 AND PENTE_EXPL(SE)    <  6.2    AND SURF_Expl(SE)     >= 5.91   , SOUS_TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 33; );
                  IF (TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 4 AND SURF_Expl(SE)     <  6.2236 AND PLUIE_EXPL(SE)    >= 2062.65, SOUS_TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 41; );
                  IF (TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 4 AND SURF_Expl(SE)     >= 6.2236 AND PLUIE_EXPL(SE)    <  2062.65, SOUS_TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 42; );
                  IF (TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 4 AND SURF_Expl(SE)     <  6.2236                                 , SOUS_TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 43; );
                  IF (TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 5 AND ALTITUDE_EXPL(SE) >= 49.23                                  , SOUS_TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 51; );
                  IF (TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 5 AND ALTITUDE_EXPL(SE) <  49.23  AND SURF_Expl(SE)     <  6.2236 , SOUS_TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 52; );
                  IF (TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 5 AND ALTITUDE_EXPL(SE) <  49.23  AND SURF_Expl(SE)     >= 6.2236 , SOUS_TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 53; );
                  IF (TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 6 AND NB_PARC_EXPL(SE)  >  4                                      , SOUS_TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 61; );
                  IF (TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 6 AND NB_PARC_EXPL(SE)  <= 4      AND ALTITUDE_EXPL(SE) >  108.08 , SOUS_TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 62; );
                  IF (TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 6 AND NB_PARC_EXPL(SE)  <= 4      AND ALTITUDE_EXPL(SE) <= 108.08 , SOUS_TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 63; );
                  IF (TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 7 AND PLUIE_EXPL(SE)    >= 2232.5                                 , SOUS_TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 71; );
                  IF (TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 7 AND PLUIE_EXPL(SE)    <  2232.5 AND NB_PARC_EXPL(SE)  >= 8      , SOUS_TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 72; );
                  IF (TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 7 AND PLUIE_EXPL(SE)    <  2232.5 AND NB_PARC_EXPL(SE)  <  8      , SOUS_TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 73; );
                  IF (TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 8 AND SURF_Expl(SE)     <  6.71   AND PENTE_EXPL(SE)    >= 12.35  , SOUS_TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 81; );
                  IF (TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 8 AND SURF_Expl(SE)     <  6.71   AND PENTE_EXPL(SE)    <  12.35  , SOUS_TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 82; );
                  IF (TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 8 AND SURF_Expl(SE)     >= 6.71                                   , SOUS_TYPE_EXPL(SE) = 83; );
         );
         STOCK_SOUS_TYPE_EXPL(SH,SE)= SOUS_TYPE_EXPL(SE);


         IF(NB_BOUCLE>0,
                  LOOP(SE,
                          IF (STOCK_TYPE_EXPL(SH,SE)= STOCK_TYPE_EXPL(SH-1,SE)            , EVAL_TYPE_Expl(SE)  =1 ELSE EVAL_TYPE_Expl(SE)  =0);
*                          IF (STOCK_TYPE_EXPL(SH,SE)= 1 AND STOCK_TYPE_EXPL(SH-1,SE)=1    , EVAL_TYPE_1_Expl(SE)=1 ELSE EVAL_TYPE_Expl(SE)  =0);
                  );
         ELSE
                  LOOP(SE,
                          EVAL_TYPE_Expl(SE)=0
                  );
         );
         STOCK_EVAL_TYPE_Expl(SH,SE)   = EVAL_TYPE_Expl(SE)  ;


         IF(NB_BOUCLE>0,
                  LOOP(SE,
                          IF (STOCK_TYPE_EXPL(SH,SE)= 1 AND STOCK_TYPE_EXPL(SH-1,SE)=1    , EVAL_TYPE_1_Expl(SE)=1);
*ELSE EVAL_TYPE_1_Expl(SE)  =0);

                          IF (STOCK_TYPE_EXPL(SH,SE)= 2 AND STOCK_TYPE_EXPL(SH-1,SE)=2    , EVAL_TYPE_2_Expl(SE)=1);
*ELSE EVAL_TYPE_2_Expl(SE)  =0);
                          IF (STOCK_TYPE_EXPL(SH,SE)= 3 AND STOCK_TYPE_EXPL(SH-1,SE)=3    , EVAL_TYPE_3_Expl(SE)=1);
*ELSE EVAL_TYPE_3_Expl(SE)  =0);
                          IF (STOCK_TYPE_EXPL(SH,SE)= 4 AND STOCK_TYPE_EXPL(SH-1,SE)=4    , EVAL_TYPE_4_Expl(SE)=1);
*ELSE EVAL_TYPE_4_Expl(SE)  =0);
                          IF (STOCK_TYPE_EXPL(SH,SE)= 5 AND STOCK_TYPE_EXPL(SH-1,SE)=5    , EVAL_TYPE_5_Expl(SE)=1);
*ELSE EVAL_TYPE_5_Expl(SE)  =0);
                          IF (STOCK_TYPE_EXPL(SH,SE)= 6 AND STOCK_TYPE_EXPL(SH-1,SE)=6    , EVAL_TYPE_6_Expl(SE)=1);
*ELSE EVAL_TYPE_6_Expl(SE)  =0);
                          IF (STOCK_TYPE_EXPL(SH,SE)= 7 AND STOCK_TYPE_EXPL(SH-1,SE)=7    , EVAL_TYPE_7_Expl(SE)=1);
*ELSE EVAL_TYPE_7_Expl(SE)  =0);
                          IF (STOCK_TYPE_EXPL(SH,SE)= 7 AND STOCK_TYPE_EXPL(SH-1,SE)=8    , EVAL_TYPE_8_Expl(SE)=1);
*ELSE EVAL_TYPE_8_Expl(SE)  =0);
                  );
         );
         STOCK_EVAL_TYPE_1_Expl(SH,SE) = EVAL_TYPE_1_Expl(SE);
         STOCK_EVAL_TYPE_2_Expl(SH,SE) = EVAL_TYPE_2_Expl(SE);
         STOCK_EVAL_TYPE_3_Expl(SH,SE) = EVAL_TYPE_3_Expl(SE);
         STOCK_EVAL_TYPE_4_Expl(SH,SE) = EVAL_TYPE_4_Expl(SE);
         STOCK_EVAL_TYPE_5_Expl(SH,SE) = EVAL_TYPE_5_Expl(SE);
         STOCK_EVAL_TYPE_6_Expl(SH,SE) = EVAL_TYPE_6_Expl(SE);
         STOCK_EVAL_TYPE_7_Expl(SH,SE) = EVAL_TYPE_7_Expl(SE);
         STOCK_EVAL_TYPE_8_Expl(SH,SE) = EVAL_TYPE_8_Expl(SE);



*Types d'exploitation: 1=Arboriculteurs, 2=Bananiers, 3=Canniers, 4=Canniers diversifiés, 5=Diversifiés, 6=Eleveurs, 7=Maraîchers, 8=Mixtes canniers-éleveurs, 0=Frichiers.
*NB: 40 exploitants sans type "frichiers"
         IF(NB_BOUCLE>1,
                 EVAL_EFF_TYPE_Gwad$NBRE_EXPL_init = (sum (SE, EVAL_TYPE_Expl(SE))/NBRE_EXPL_init)*100;
                 EVAL_EFF_TYPE_1_Gwad$STOCK_EFF_TYPE_1_Gwad(SH-1)    = (sum (SE, EVAL_TYPE_1_Expl(SE))/STOCK_EFF_TYPE_1_Gwad(SH-1))*100;
                 EVAL_EFF_TYPE_2_Gwad$STOCK_EFF_TYPE_2_Gwad(SH-1)    = (sum (SE, EVAL_TYPE_2_Expl(SE))/STOCK_EFF_TYPE_2_Gwad(SH-1))*100;
                 EVAL_EFF_TYPE_3_Gwad$STOCK_EFF_TYPE_3_Gwad(SH-1)    = (sum (SE, EVAL_TYPE_3_Expl(SE))/STOCK_EFF_TYPE_3_Gwad(SH-1))*100;
                 EVAL_EFF_TYPE_4_Gwad$STOCK_EFF_TYPE_4_Gwad(SH-1)    = (sum (SE, EVAL_TYPE_4_Expl(SE))/STOCK_EFF_TYPE_4_Gwad(SH-1))*100;
                 EVAL_EFF_TYPE_5_Gwad$STOCK_EFF_TYPE_5_Gwad(SH-1)    = (sum (SE, EVAL_TYPE_5_Expl(SE))/STOCK_EFF_TYPE_5_Gwad(SH-1))*100;
                 EVAL_EFF_TYPE_6_Gwad$STOCK_EFF_TYPE_6_Gwad(SH-1)    = (sum (SE, EVAL_TYPE_6_Expl(SE))/STOCK_EFF_TYPE_6_Gwad(SH-1))*100;
                 EVAL_EFF_TYPE_7_Gwad$STOCK_EFF_TYPE_7_Gwad(SH-1)    = (sum (SE, EVAL_TYPE_7_Expl(SE))/STOCK_EFF_TYPE_7_Gwad(SH-1))*100;
                 EVAL_EFF_TYPE_8_Gwad$STOCK_EFF_TYPE_8_Gwad(SH-1)    = (sum (SE, EVAL_TYPE_8_Expl(SE))/STOCK_EFF_TYPE_8_Gwad(SH-1))*100;
         );
         STOCK_EVAL_EFF_TYPE_Gwad(SH)      = EVAL_EFF_TYPE_Gwad;
         STOCK_EVAL_EFF_TYPE_1_Gwad(SH)    = EVAL_EFF_TYPE_1_Gwad;
         STOCK_EVAL_EFF_TYPE_2_Gwad(SH)    = EVAL_EFF_TYPE_2_Gwad;
         STOCK_EVAL_EFF_TYPE_3_Gwad(SH)    = EVAL_EFF_TYPE_3_Gwad;
         STOCK_EVAL_EFF_TYPE_4_Gwad(SH)    = EVAL_EFF_TYPE_4_Gwad;
         STOCK_EVAL_EFF_TYPE_5_Gwad(SH)    = EVAL_EFF_TYPE_5_Gwad;
         STOCK_EVAL_EFF_TYPE_6_Gwad(SH)    = EVAL_EFF_TYPE_6_Gwad;
         STOCK_EVAL_EFF_TYPE_7_Gwad(SH)    = EVAL_EFF_TYPE_7_Gwad;
         STOCK_EVAL_EFF_TYPE_8_Gwad(SH)    = EVAL_EFF_TYPE_8_Gwad;


* 1=Arboriculteurs, 2=Bananiers, 3=Canniers,   4=Canniers diversifiés,
* 5=Diversifiés,    6=Eleveurs,  7=Maraîchers, 8=Mixtes canniers-éleveurs, 0=Frichiers.
* les valeurs de AVERS détérminées de manière iterative    de 0 à 3 avec 3 très averse  ; 0 ne diminue pas la MB, 3 bcp (linéaire)
*  var_rdt variation de rdt et de prix=> variation de mb entre 0 et 1 0=aucne variation, 1 =

         LOOP(SE,
                  IF(STOCK_TYPE_EXPL("init",SE) = 1,    AVERS(SE)= 1.30);
                  IF(STOCK_TYPE_EXPL("init",SE) = 2,    AVERS(SE)= 1.20);
                  IF(STOCK_TYPE_EXPL("init",SE) = 3,    AVERS(SE)= 0.30);
                  IF(STOCK_TYPE_EXPL("init",SE) = 4,    AVERS(SE)= 1.40);
                  IF(STOCK_TYPE_EXPL("init",SE) = 5,    AVERS(SE)= 0.55);
                  IF(STOCK_TYPE_EXPL("init",SE) = 6,    AVERS(SE)= 2.40);
                  IF(STOCK_TYPE_EXPL("init",SE) = 7,    AVERS(SE)= 0.00);
                  IF(STOCK_TYPE_EXPL("init",SE) = 8,    AVERS(SE)= 2.30);
                  IF(STOCK_TYPE_EXPL_BIS("init",SE)=41, AVERS(SE)= 0.50);
                  IF(STOCK_TYPE_EXPL_BIS("init",SE)=42, AVERS(SE)= 1.60);





         );
         STOCK_AVERS(SH,SE) = AVERS(SE);


         PROD_CS_MG =       sum(SP, sum(SC_CS_MG, ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC_CS_MG) * Rdt_Cult(SC_CS_MG,SH)));
         STOCK_PROD_CS_MG(SH) = PROD_CS_MG;

         PROD_CS_GWAD_TOT = sum(SP, sum(SC_CS,    ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC_CS)    * Rdt_Cult(SC_CS   ,SH)));
         STOCK_PROD_CS_GWAD_TOT(SH) = PROD_CS_GWAD_TOT;

         PROD_CS_GWAD_CONT= PROD_CS_GWAD_TOT - PROD_CS_MG;
         STOCK_PROD_CS_GWAD_CONT(SH) = PROD_CS_GWAD_CONT;


         TONNE_SUCRE  = sum((SP,SC_CS),     ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC_CS)* Rdt_Cult(SC_CS,SH)* RS_Cult(SC_CS,SH) )*RDT_INDUS_SUCRE;
         STOCK_TONNE_SUCRE(SH)  = TONNE_SUCRE;

         TONNE_BANANE = sum((SP,SC_BAN_EX), ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC_BAN_EX) * Rdt_Cult(SC_BAN_EX,SH));
         STOCK_TONNE_BANANE(SH) = TONNE_BANANE;


         LOOP((SP,SC),
                 IF (STOCK_ASSOL_Parc(SH,SP,SC) = STOCK_ASSOL_Parc(SH-1,SP,SC) AND STOCK_ASSOL_Parc("init",SP,SC)>0, EVAL_ASSOL_Parc(SP)=1
                 );
         );
         STOCK_EVAL_ASSOL_Parc(SH,SP) = EVAL_ASSOL_Parc(SP);


         EVAL_EFF_Usage_Gwad = (sum (SP, EVAL_ASSOL_Parc(SP))/NBRE_PARC_init)*100;
         STOCK_EVAL_EFF_Usage_Gwad(SH) = EVAL_EFF_Usage_Gwad;

         EVAL_EFF_Type_Gwad = (sum(SE, EVAL_TYPE_Expl(SE)) / NBRE_EXPL_init ) * 100;
         STOCK_EVAL_EFF_Type_Gwad(SH) = EVAL_EFF_Type_Gwad;

         EVAL_ASSOL_Reg(SR,SC)$STOCK_ASSOL_Reg(SH-1,SR,SC) = STOCK_ASSOL_Reg(SH,SR,SC)/STOCK_ASSOL_Reg(SH-1,SR,SC);
         STOCK_EVAL_ASSOL_Reg(SH,SR,SC)= EVAL_ASSOL_Reg(SR,SC);

         EVAL_ASSOL_Gwad(SC)$STOCK_ASSOL_Gwad(SH-1,SC) = ABS(1 -(STOCK_ASSOL_Gwad(SH,SC)/STOCK_ASSOL_Gwad(SH-1,SC))*100);
         STOCK_EVAL_ASSOL_Gwad(SH,SC)= EVAL_ASSOL_Gwad(SC);

*
***************************************************************************************************************************************************
*DIMENSIONS       SUSTAINABILITY OBJECTIVES                              INDICATORS
***************************************************************************************************************************************************
*Economic         Improving the agricultural added value                  1°)   Agricultural added value
*                                                                         2°)   Repartition of revenue among the farm population
*                 Increasing the independence from subsidies              3°)   Total amount of subsidies
*                                                                         4°)   Mean efficiency of farmers

*Social           Reaching food self-sufficiency                          5°)   Ration of carbohydrates produces over needs
*                                                                         6°)   Ratio of fat produced over needs
*                                                                         7°)   Ration of proteins produced over needs
*                 Producing local energy from agriculture                 8°)   Potential of energy produced by crops
*                 Contributing to employment                              9°)   Total needs of workforce
*                 Insuring safety of locally produced foodstuff          10°)   Area of risk of contamination of food crops

*Environmental    Improving the state of biodiversity                    11°)   Mean quantity of toxicity in fields located in ZNIEFF areas
*                 Enhancing water quality                                12°)   Number of rivers potentially polluted
*                                                                        13°)   Number of water catchments potentially polluted
*                                                                        14°)   Number of abstraction water sources potentially polluted
*                                                                        15°)   Amount of water used for irrigation
*                 Protecting soil quality                                16°)   Percentage of the area potentially eroded due to farming practices
*                 Decreasing the contribution to climate change          17°)   Overall emissions of CO2 from farming activities
*                 Improving the diversity of agricultural landscapes     18°)   Diversity of crops across landscape
***************************************************************************************************************************************************

****************************************************************************************************************************
* INDICATEUR N°1: Agricultural added value:  "Improving the agricultural added value" oooooooooooooooooooooooooooooooooooooo
****************************************************************************************************************************
         REV_Parc(SP)           = sum(SC, ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)*MB_HA_Cult(SC));
         STOCK_REV_Parc(SH,SP)  = REV_Parc(SP);

         REV_Cult(SC)           = sum(SP, ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)*MB_HA_Cult(SC));
         STOCK_REV_Cult(SH,SC)  = REV_Cult(SC);

         REV_Expl(SE)           = sum(SP$Expl_Parc(SE,SP), REV_Parc(SP));
         STOCK_REV_Expl(SH,SE)  = REV_Expl(SE);

         REV_Reg(SR)            = sum(SP$Reg_Parc(SR,SP),  REV_Parc(SP));
         STOCK_REV_Reg(SH,SR)   = REV_Reg(SR);

         REV_Gwad               = sum(SP,                  REV_Parc(SP));
         STOCK_REV_Gwad(SH)     = REV_Gwad;

         REV_Moy_ha_Gwad = sum(SP,REV_Parc(SP))/ASSOL_GWAD_tot_TOTAL;
         STOCK_REV_Moy_ha_Gwad(SH)= REV_Moy_ha_Gwad;

         REV_Expl_Moy_Gwad = sum(SE,REV_Expl(SE))/NBRE_EXPL_init;
         STOCK_REV_Expl_Moy_Gwad(SH)= REV_Expl_Moy_Gwad;
