         SUB_POSEI_Parc(SP) = sum(SC, ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC) * SUB_POSEI_Ha_Cult(SC));
         STOCK_SUB_POSEI_Parc(SH,SP)    = SUB_POSEI_Parc(SP);

         SUB_POSEI_Cult(SC) = sum(SP, ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC) * SUB_POSEI_Ha_Cult(SC));
         STOCK_SUB_POSEI_Cult(SH,SC)    = SUB_POSEI_Cult(SC);

         SUB_POSEI_Expl(SE) = sum(SP$Expl_Parc(SE,SP),    SUB_POSEI_Parc(SP));
         STOCK_SUB_POSEI_Expl(SH,SE)    = SUB_POSEI_Expl(SE);

         SUB_POSEI_Reg(SR)  = sum(SP$Reg_Parc(SR,SP),     SUB_POSEI_Parc(SP));
         STOCK_SUB_POSEI_Reg(SH,SR)     = SUB_POSEI_Reg(SR);

         SUB_POSEI_Gwad     = sum(SP,                     SUB_POSEI_Parc(SP));
         STOCK_SUB_POSEI_Gwad(SH)       = SUB_POSEI_Gwad;


         SUB_NAT_Parc(SP)   = sum(SC, ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC) * SUB_NAT_Ha_Cult(SC));
         STOCK_SUB_NAT_Parc(SH,SP)      = SUB_NAT_Parc(SP);

         SUB_NAT_Cult(SC)   = sum(SP, ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC) * SUB_NAT_Ha_Cult(SC));
         STOCK_SUB_NAT_Cult(SH,SC)      = SUB_NAT_Cult(SC);

         SUB_NAT_Expl(SE)   = sum(SP$Expl_Parc(SE,SP),    SUB_NAT_Parc(SP));
         STOCK_SUB_NAT_Expl(SH,SE)      = SUB_NAT_Expl(SE);

         SUB_NAT_Reg(SR)    = sum(SP$Reg_Parc(SR,SP),     SUB_NAT_Parc(SP));
         STOCK_SUB_NAT_Reg(SH,SR)       = SUB_NAT_Reg(SR);

         SUB_NAT_Gwad       = sum(SP,                     SUB_NAT_Parc(SP));
         STOCK_SUB_NAT_Gwad(SH)         = SUB_NAT_Gwad;


         SUB_PDRG_Parc(SP)  = sum(SC, ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC) * SUB_PDRG_Ha_Cult(SC));
         STOCK_SUB_PDRG_Parc(SH,SP)     = SUB_PDRG_Parc(SP);

         SUB_PDRG_Cult(SC)  = sum(SP, ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC) * SUB_PDRG_Ha_Cult(SC));
         STOCK_SUB_PDRG_Cult(SH,SC)     = SUB_PDRG_Cult(SC);

         SUB_PDRG_Expl(SE)  = sum(SP$Expl_Parc(SE,SP),    SUB_PDRG_Parc(SP));
         STOCK_SUB_PDRG_Expl(SH,SE)     = SUB_PDRG_Expl(SE);

         SUB_PDRG_Reg(SR)   = sum(SP$Reg_Parc(SR,SP),     SUB_PDRG_Parc(SP));
         STOCK_SUB_PDRG_Reg(SH,SR)      = SUB_PDRG_Reg(SR);

         SUB_PDRG_Gwad      = sum(SP,                     SUB_PDRG_Parc(SP));
         STOCK_SUB_PDRG_Gwad(SH)        = SUB_PDRG_Gwad;


         SUB_TOT_Parc(SP)   = sum(SC, ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC) * SUB_TOT_Ha_Cult(SC));
         STOCK_SUB_TOT_Parc(SH,SP)      = SUB_TOT_Parc(SP);

         SUB_TOT_Cult(SC)   = sum(SP, ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC) * SUB_TOT_Ha_Cult(SC));
         STOCK_SUB_TOT_Cult(SH,SC)      = SUB_TOT_Cult(SC);

         SUB_TOT_Expl(SE)   = sum(SP$Expl_Parc(SE,SP),    SUB_TOT_Parc(SP));
         STOCK_SUB_TOT_Expl(SH,SE)      = SUB_TOT_Expl(SE);

         SUB_TOT_Reg(SR)    = sum(SP$Reg_Parc(SR,SP),     SUB_TOT_Parc(SP));
         STOCK_SUB_TOT_Reg(SH,SR)       = SUB_TOT_Reg(SR);

         SUB_TOT_Gwad       = sum(SP,                     SUB_TOT_Parc(SP));
         STOCK_SUB_TOT_Gwad(SH)         = SUB_TOT_Gwad;

         SUB_TOT_Moy_ha     = SUB_TOT_Gwad /  ASSOL_GWAD_tot_TOTAL;
         STOCK_SUB_TOT_Moy_ha(SH)       = SUB_TOT_Moy_ha;

****************************************************************************************************************************
*INDICATEUR n°4: Mean efficiency of farmers  "Increasing the independence from subsidies"  ooooooooooooooooooooooooooooooooo
****************************************************************************************************************************
         EFFI_Ha_Cult(SC)$(MB_Ha_Cult(SC))  = (CV_Ha_Cult(SC)/MB_Ha_Cult(SC))*100     ;
         STOCK_EFFI_Ha_Cult(SH,SC)          = EFFI_Ha_Cult(SC);

         EFFI_Ha_Parc(SP)                   =  sum(SC, EFFI_Ha_Cult(SC) * ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC));
         STOCK_EFFI_Ha_Parc(SH,SP)          = EFFI_Ha_Parc(SP);

         EFFI_Expl(SE)                      = (sum(SP$Expl_Parc(SE,SP), EFFI_HA_Parc(SP)) ) / SURF_Expl(SE)   ;
         STOCK_EFFI_Expl(SH,SE)             = EFFI_Expl(SE)  ;

         EFFI_Gwad                          = (sum(SE, EFFI_Expl(SE))) / NBRE_EXPL_init;
         STOCK_EFFI_Gwad(SH)                = EFFI_Gwad;


*****************************************************************************************************************************
*INDICATEUR n°5: Ratio of carbohydrates produces over needs  "Reaching food self-sufficiency"  oooooooooooooooooooooooooooooo
*INDICATEUR n°6: Ratio of lipids produces over needs  "Reaching food self-sufficiency"         oooooooooooooooooooooooooooooo
*INDICATEUR n°7: Ratio of proteins produces over needs  "Reaching food self-sufficiency"       oooooooooooooooooooooooooooooo
*****************************************************************************************************************************

*ooooooo Calcul de la quantité de nutriments produite par l'assolement pour chaque catégorie de nutriments
         NUTRI_Parc(SVAR_NUT,SP) = sum(SC, ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)*NUTRI_Cult(SVAR_NUT,SC)*Rdt_Cult(SC,SH)) ;
         STOCK_NUTRI_Parc(SH,SVAR_NUT,SP) = NUTRI_Parc(SVAR_NUT,SP);

         NUTRI_Gwad(SVAR_NUT) = sum(SP, sum(SC,ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC) * NUTRI_Cult(SVAR_NUT,SC) * Rdt_Cult(SC,SH)))
                                  + NUTRI_ALIM(SVAR_NUT,"PECHE") * NUTRI_ALIM("Q_tot","PECHE");
         STOCK_NUTRI_Gwad(SH,SVAR_NUT) = NUTRI_Gwad(SVAR_NUT);


*ooooooo Calcul de la quantité d'individus moyens que l'on peut alimenter avec l'assolement initial pour chaque catégorie de nutriment
         Q_IND_MOY_ALIM(SVAR_NUT)$NUTRI_ALIM(SVAR_NUT,"Ind_Moy")= NUTRI_Gwad(SVAR_NUT) / NUTRI_ALIM(SVAR_NUT,"Ind_Moy");
         STOCK_Q_IND_MOY_ALIM(SH,SVAR_NUT) =  Q_IND_MOY_ALIM(SVAR_NUT);

         Q_IND_MOY_ALIM_MIN = smin((SVAR_NUT),   Q_Ind_Moy_Alim(SVAR_NUT) );
         STOCK_Q_IND_MOY_ALIM_MIN(SH) =  Q_IND_MOY_ALIM_MIN;


*ooooooo Calcul des besoins alimentaire de la population guadeloupéenne (en individus moyens)
         BESOIN_ALIM_Gwad(SVAR_NUT) = NUTRI_ALIM(SVAR_NUT,"Ind_Moy")*NUTRI_ALIM("Q_Tot","Ind_Moy");
         STOCK_BESOIN_ALIM_Gwad(SH,SVAR_NUT) = BESOIN_ALIM_Gwad(SVAR_NUT);

         RATIO_PROD_BESOIN(SVAR_NUT) = Q_IND_MOY_ALIM(SVAR_NUT) /  NUTRI_ALIM("Q_Tot","Ind_Moy")   ;
         STOCK_RATIO_PROD_BESOIN(SH,SVAR_NUT) = RATIO_PROD_BESOIN(SVAR_NUT);


****************************************************************************************************************************
*INDICATEUR n°8: Potential of energy produced by crops  "Producing local energy from agriculture"  ooooooooooooooooooooooooo
****************************************************************************************************************************
         ELEC_Ha_cult(SC)          = Data_Cult("MW",SC) * Rdt_Cult(SC,SH);
         STOCK_ELEC_Ha_cult(SH,SC) = ELEC_Ha_cult(SC);

         ELEC_Parc(SP)             = sum(SC,                  ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)*ELEC_Ha_cult(SC));
         STOCK_ELEC_Parc(SH,SP)    = ELEC_Parc(SP);

         ELEC_Cult(SC)             = sum(SP,                  ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)*ELEC_Ha_cult(SC));
         STOCK_ELEC_Cult(SH,SC)    = ELEC_Cult(SC);

         ELEC_Expl(SE)             = sum(SP$Expl_Parc(SE,SP), ELEC_Parc(SP) );
         STOCK_ELEC_Expl(SH,SE)    = ELEC_Expl(SE);

         ELEC_Reg(SR)              = sum(SP$Reg_Parc(SR,SP),  ELEC_Parc(SP));
         STOCK_ELEC_Reg(SH,SR)     = ELEC_Reg(SR);

         ELEC_Gwad                 = sum(SP,                  ELEC_Parc(SP) );
         STOCK_ELEC_Gwad(SH)       = ELEC_Gwad;


****************************************************************************************************************************
*INDICATEUR n°9: Total needs of workforce  "Contributing to employment"  ooooooooooooooooooooooooooooooooooooooooooooooooooo
****************************************************************************************************************************
         MO_Parc(SP)              = sum(SC, ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)*MO_Ha_Cult(SC)) ;
         STOCK_MO_Parc(SH,SP )    = MO_Parc(SP);

         MO_Cult(SC)              = sum(SP, ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)*MO_Ha_Cult(SC));
         STOCK_MO_Cult(SH,SC )    = MO_Cult(SC);

         MO_Expl(SE)              = sum(SP$Expl_Parc(SE,SP), MO_Parc(SP) );
         STOCK_MO_Expl(SH,SE)     = MO_Expl(SE);

         MO_Reg(SR)               = sum(SP$Reg_Parc(SR,SP),  MO_Parc(SP));
         STOCK_MO_Reg(SH,SR)      = MO_Reg(SR);

         MO_Gwad                  = sum(SP,                  MO_Parc(SP))  ;
         STOCK_MO_Gwad(SH)        = MO_Gwad;

         MO_Gwad_Moy_ha           = MO_Gwad /  ASSOL_GWAD_tot_TOTAL;
         STOCK_MO_Gwad_Moy_ha(SH) = MO_Gwad_Moy_ha;

****************************************************************************************************************************
*INDICATEUR n°10: Area of risk of contamination of food crops  "Insuring safety of locally produced foodstuff"  oooooooooooo
****************************************************************************************************************************
         RCLD_parc(SP)$SURF_Parc(SP) = sum(SC, Data_Cult("CLD",SC)*  (ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC) / SURF_Parc(SP)));
         STOCK_RCLD_parc(SH,SP)   = RCLD_parc(SP);

         LOOP(SP,
* si risque culture très important et risque parcelle peu important, important ou très important, alors parcelle à production risquée
                  IF ( RCLD_parc(SP) =1 AND Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"RISQUE_CLD")<=3                                , NV_CLD_parc(SP)=1);
* si risque culture important et risque parcelle important ou très important, alors parcelle à production risquée
                  IF ( RCLD_parc(SP) =2 AND Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"RISQUE_CLD")<=2                                , NV_CLD_parc(SP)=1);
* si risque culture peu important, risque parcelle important ou très important, et sol ferralitique ou nitisol, alors parcelle à production risquée
                  IF ( RCLD_parc(SP) =3 AND Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"RISQUE_CLD")<=2 AND (Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"TYPE_SOL")=2 OR Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"TYPE_SOL")=4), NV_CLD_parc(SP)=1);
* si risque culture peu important, risque parcelle important ou très important, et vertisol ou andosol ou autre, alors parcelle à production risquée
                  IF ( RCLD_parc(SP) =3 AND Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"RISQUE_CLD")=1 AND  (Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"TYPE_SOL")=1 OR Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"TYPE_SOL")=3 OR Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"TYPE_SOL")=5), NV_CLD_parc(SP)=1);
         );
         STOCK_NV_CLD_Parc(SH,SP) = NV_CLD_parc(SP);

         RCLD_Gwad = sum(SP, NV_CLD_parc(SP)* Surf_parc(SP) );
         STOCK_RCLD_gwad(SH)      = RCLD_Gwad;


****************************************************************************************************************************
*INDICATEUR n°11: Mean quantity of toxicity in fields located in ZNIEFF areas  "Improving the state of biodiversity"  oooooo
****************************************************************************************************************************
*http://www.inspq.qc.ca/pdf/publications/1504_IndicRisquesPesticides_2eEdition.pdf
*Norwegian Agricultural Inspection Service (NAIS),2004. Pesticide Risk Indicators for Health and Environment – Norway.

         LOOP(SP,
                  IF ( Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"ZNIEFF1T") + Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"ZNIEFF2T") >= 1, PRES_ZNIEFFS(SP)= 1) ;
                  IF ( Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"ZNIEFF1T") + Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"ZNIEFF2T") <  1, PRES_ZNIEFFS(SP)= 0 );
         );
         STOCK_PRES_ZNIEFFS(SH,SP)           =  PRES_ZNIEFFS(SP);

         SURF_ZNIEFF = sum(SP$(PRES_ZNIEFFS(SP)=1), SURF_PARC(SP) ) ;
         STOCK_SURF_ZNIEFF(SH)               =  SURF_ZNIEFF;

         LOAD_INDEX_OTK_cult(SC,SK)$(Data_OTK(SK,"BIRD"))=
                  Matrice_OTK_Cult(SK, SC)$(Data_OTK(SK,"AMORTI")=0)*((Data_OTK(SK,"DOSE")*Data_OTK(SK,"QMA"))/Data_OTK(SK,"BIRD"))
               + (Matrice_OTK_Cult(SK, SC)$(Data_OTK(SK,"AMORTI")=1)*((Data_OTK(SK,"DOSE")*Data_OTK(SK,"QMA"))/Data_OTK(SK,"BIRD")) /  Duree_Plant_Cult(SC,SH) )
              / Duree_Cycle_Cult(SC,SH)*12;
         STOCK_LOAD_INDEX_OTK_Cult(SH,SC,SK) =  LOAD_INDEX_OTK_cult(SC,SK);

         LOAD_INDEX_Cult(SC) = sum(SK, LOAD_INDEX_OTK_cult(SC,SK));
         STOCK_LOAD_INDEX_Cult(SH,SC)        =  LOAD_INDEX_Cult(SC);

         LOAD_INDEX_Parc(SP)$(PRES_ZNIEFFS(SP)=1)= sum( SC, LOAD_INDEX_Cult(SC) * ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)) ;
         STOCK_LOAD_INDEX_Parc(SH,SP)        =  LOAD_INDEX_Parc(SP);

         LOAD_INDEX_Expl(SE)= sum(SP$(Expl_Parc(SE,SP) and (PRES_ZNIEFFS(SP)=1)), LOAD_INDEX_Parc(SP)) ;
         STOCK_LOAD_INDEX_Expl(SH,SE) = LOAD_INDEX_Expl(SE);

         LOAD_INDEX_Reg(SR)=  sum(SP$(Reg_Parc(SR,SP)  and (PRES_ZNIEFFS(SP)=1)), LOAD_INDEX_Parc(SP)) ;
         STOCK_LOAD_INDEX_Reg(SH,SR) = LOAD_INDEX_Reg(SR);

         LOAD_INDEX_Gwad    = sum(SP$(PRES_ZNIEFFS(SP)=1), LOAD_INDEX_Parc(SP) );
         STOCK_LOAD_INDEX_Gwad(SH)           =  LOAD_INDEX_Gwad;

         LOAD_INDEX_ZNIEFF$(SURF_ZNIEFF) = sum(SP$(PRES_ZNIEFFS(SP)=1), LOAD_INDEX_Gwad / SURF_ZNIEFF );
         STOCK_LOAD_INDEX_ZNIEFF(SH)         =  LOAD_INDEX_ZNIEFF;



***************** R_PEST NOUVEAU ***********************************************

$include C:\FichierII\MOSAICA\MOSAICA_V3\MOSAICA_3.5\MODELE\R_PEST_NEW.GMS


*ooooooo Calcul de la DT50 moyenne par culture pondérée par les quantités de produits phytosanitaires utilisés (en jours/ha/an) moyenne, année de plantation comprise (pour le calcul de R_PEST)

* La persistance dans le sol ou temps de demi-vie (DT50) est évaluée par le temps de dégradation ou la dissipation de 50 % de la substance active présente dans le sol. La base de données « phytosanitaires »
*renseigne la persistance (DT50) mesurée au champ. Elle est exprimée en jours.

         DT50_OTK_Cult(SC,SK)$((        Matrice_OTK_Cult(SK, SC)$(Data_OTK(SK,"AMORTI")=0)*Data_OTK(SK,"DOSE")
                                     + (Matrice_OTK_Cult(SK, SC)$(Data_OTK(SK,"AMORTI")=1)*Data_OTK(SK,"DOSE") )/Duree_Plant_Cult(SC,SH)
                                )/  Duree_Cycle_Cult(SC,SH)*12
                               )
                                =((     Matrice_OTK_Cult(SK, SC)$(Data_OTK(SK,"AMORTI")=0)*Data_OTK(SK,"DOSE")*Data_OTK(SK,"DT50")
                                     + (Matrice_OTK_Cult(SK, SC)$(Data_OTK(SK,"AMORTI")=1)*Data_OTK(SK,"DOSE")*Data_OTK(SK,"DT50") )/Duree_Plant_Cult(SC,SH)
                                )/  Duree_Cycle_Cult(SC,SH)*12 )/
                               ((       Matrice_OTK_Cult(SK, SC)$(Data_OTK(SK,"AMORTI")=0)*Data_OTK(SK,"DOSE")
                                     + (Matrice_OTK_Cult(SK, SC)$(Data_OTK(SK,"AMORTI")=1)*Data_OTK(SK,"DOSE") )/Duree_Plant_Cult(SC,SH)
                               )/   Duree_Cycle_Cult(SC,SH)*12
                               );
         STOCK_DT50_OTK_Cult(SH,SC,SK)= DT50_OTK_Cult(SC,SK);

         DT50_Cult(SC)= sum(SK, DT50_OTK_Cult(SC,SK));
         STOCK_DT50_Cult(SH,SC)= DT50_Cult(SC);


*ooooooo Calcul de la dose maximum admise par un humain moyenne par culture pondérée par les quantités de produits phytosanitaires utilisés (en jours/ha/an) moyenne, année de plantation comprise (pour le calcul de R_PEST)
         ADI_OTK_cult(SC,SK)$((        Matrice_OTK_Cult(SK, SC)$(Data_OTK(SK,"AMORTI")=0)*Data_OTK(SK,"DOSE")
                                    + (Matrice_OTK_Cult(SK, SC)$(Data_OTK(SK,"AMORTI")=1)*Data_OTK(SK,"DOSE") )/Duree_Plant_Cult(SC,SH)
                               )/  Duree_Cycle_Cult(SC,SH)*12
                               )
                               =((     Matrice_OTK_Cult(SK, SC)$(Data_OTK(SK,"AMORTI")=0)*Data_OTK(SK,"DOSE")*Data_OTK(SK,"ADI")
                                    + (Matrice_OTK_Cult(SK, SC)$(Data_OTK(SK,"AMORTI")=1)*Data_OTK(SK,"DOSE")*Data_OTK(SK,"ADI") )/Duree_Plant_Cult(SC,SH)
                                )/  Duree_Cycle_Cult(SC,SH)*12 )/
                              ((       Matrice_OTK_Cult(SK, SC)$(Data_OTK(SK,"AMORTI")=0)*Data_OTK(SK,"DOSE")
                                    + (Matrice_OTK_Cult(SK, SC)$(Data_OTK(SK,"AMORTI")=1)*Data_OTK(SK,"DOSE") )/Duree_Plant_Cult(SC,SH)
                               )/   Duree_Cycle_Cult(SC,SH)*12
                              );
         STOCK_ADI_OTK_cult(SH,SC,SK)= ADI_OTK_cult(SC,SK);

         ADI_Cult(SC)= sum(SK, ADI_OTK_cult(SC,SK));
         STOCK_ADI_Cult(SH,SC)= ADI_Cult(SC);


*ooooooo Calcul de la dose maximum admise par un poisson moyenne par culture pondérée par les quantités de produits phytosanitaires utilisés (en jours/ha/an) moyenne, année de plantation comprise (pour le calcul de R_PEST)
         AQUATOX_OTK_cult(SC,SK)$((    Matrice_OTK_Cult(SK, SC)$(Data_OTK(SK,"AMORTI")=0)*Data_OTK(SK,"DOSE")
                                    + (Matrice_OTK_Cult(SK, SC)$(Data_OTK(SK,"AMORTI")=1)*Data_OTK(SK,"DOSE") )/Duree_Plant_Cult(SC,SH)
                               )/  Duree_Cycle_Cult(SC,SH)*12
                               )
                               =((     Matrice_OTK_Cult(SK, SC)$(Data_OTK(SK,"AMORTI")=0)*Data_OTK(SK,"DOSE")*Data_OTK(SK,"AQUATOX")
                                    + (Matrice_OTK_Cult(SK, SC)$(Data_OTK(SK,"AMORTI")=1)*Data_OTK(SK,"DOSE")*Data_OTK(SK,"AQUATOX") )/Duree_Plant_Cult(SC,SH)
                                )/  Duree_Cycle_Cult(SC,SH)*12 )/
                              ((       Matrice_OTK_Cult(SK, SC)$(Data_OTK(SK,"AMORTI")=0)*Data_OTK(SK,"DOSE")
                                    + (Matrice_OTK_Cult(SK, SC)$(Data_OTK(SK,"AMORTI")=1)*Data_OTK(SK,"DOSE") )/Duree_Plant_Cult(SC,SH)
                               )/   Duree_Cycle_Cult(SC,SH)*12
                              );
         STOCK_AQUATOX_OTK_cult(SH,SC,SK)= AQUATOX_OTK_cult(SC,SK);

         AQUATOX_Cult(SC)= sum(SK, AQUATOX_OTK_cult(SC,SK));
         STOCK_AQUATOX_Cult(SH,SC)= AQUATOX_Cult(SC);


*ooooooo Calcul GUS moyen par culture pondérée par les quantités de produits phytosanitaires utilisés (en jours/ha/an) moyenne, année de plantation comprise (pour le calcul de R_PEST)
         GUS_OTK_cult(SC,SK)$((        Matrice_OTK_Cult(SK, SC)$(Data_OTK(SK,"AMORTI")=0)*Data_OTK(SK,"DOSE")
                                     + (Matrice_OTK_Cult(SK, SC)$(Data_OTK(SK,"AMORTI")=1)*Data_OTK(SK,"DOSE") )/Duree_Plant_Cult(SC,SH)
                                )/  Duree_Cycle_Cult(SC,SH)*12
                               )
                                =((     Matrice_OTK_Cult(SK, SC)$(Data_OTK(SK,"AMORTI")=0)*Data_OTK(SK,"DOSE")*Data_OTK(SK,"GUS")
                                     + (Matrice_OTK_Cult(SK, SC)$(Data_OTK(SK,"AMORTI")=1)*Data_OTK(SK,"DOSE")*Data_OTK(SK,"GUS") )/Duree_Plant_Cult(SC,SH)
                                )/  Duree_Cycle_Cult(SC,SH)*12 )/
                               ((       Matrice_OTK_Cult(SK, SC)$(Data_OTK(SK,"AMORTI")=0)*Data_OTK(SK,"DOSE")
                                     + (Matrice_OTK_Cult(SK, SC)$(Data_OTK(SK,"AMORTI")=1)*Data_OTK(SK,"DOSE") )/Duree_Plant_Cult(SC,SH)
                               )/   Duree_Cycle_Cult(SC,SH)*12
                               );
         STOCK_GUS_OTK_cult(SH,SC,SK)= GUS_OTK_cult(SC,SK);

         GUS_Cult(SC)= sum(SK, GUS_OTK_cult(SC,SK));
         STOCK_GUS_Cult(SH,SC)= GUS_Cult(SC);


*ooooooo Calcul de la charge en matière active par culture (en kg de MA/ha/an) moyenne, année de plantation comprise (pour le calcul de R_PEST)
         QMA_ha_OTK_cult(SC,SK)= (  Matrice_OTK_Cult(SK, SC)$(Data_OTK(SK,"AMORTI")=0)*Data_OTK(SK,"DOSE")*Data_OTK(SK,"QMA")
                                 + (Matrice_OTK_Cult(SK, SC)$(Data_OTK(SK,"AMORTI")=1)*Data_OTK(SK,"DOSE")*Data_OTK(SK,"QMA") )/Duree_Plant_Cult(SC,SH)
                                )/ Duree_Cycle_Cult(SC,SH)*12;
         STOCK_QMA_ha_OTK_cult(SH,SC,SK)= QMA_ha_OTK_cult(SC,SK);

         QMA_Ha_Cult(SC)= sum(SK, QMA_ha_OTK_cult(SC,SK));
         STOCK_QMA_Ha_Cult(SH,SC)= QMA_Ha_Cult(SC);


*ooooooo Calcul de R-PEST par parcelle ooooooooooooooooooooooooooooooooooooooooo

         LOOP(SC,
                  IF (QMA_Ha_Cult(SC) >  R_Tixier("SEUIL_DOSE_CULT","DEFAVORABLE"), DOSE_defav(SC)=1;
                  );
                  IF (QMA_Ha_Cult(SC) <  R_Tixier("SEUIL_DOSE_CULT","FAVORABLE"),   DOSE_defav(SC)=0;
                  );
                  IF (QMA_Ha_Cult(SC) <= R_Tixier("SEUIL_DOSE_CULT","DEFAVORABLE") and (QMA_Ha_Cult(SC)>R_Tixier("SEUIL_DOSE_CULT","FAVORABLE")),
                        DOSE_defav(SC)$((R_Tixier("SEUIL_DOSE_CULT","DEFAVORABLE") - R_Tixier("SEUIL_DOSE_CULT","FAVORABLE")))
                     =(QMA_Ha_Cult(SC) - R_Tixier("SEUIL_DOSE_CULT","FAVORABLE")) /((R_Tixier("SEUIL_DOSE_CULT","DEFAVORABLE") - R_Tixier("SEUIL_DOSE_CULT","FAVORABLE")));
                  );
         );
         STOCK_DOSE_defav(SH,SC)= DOSE_defav(SC);

         DOSE_fav(SC)= 1-DOSE_defav(SC);
         STOCK_DOSE_fav(SH,SC)= DOSE_fav(SC);



         LOOP(SP,
                  IF (Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"RUI_PARC") <  R_Tixier("SEUIL_RUI_PARC","FAVORABLE"),   RUI_defav(SP)=0;
                  );
                  IF (Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"RUI_PARC") >  R_Tixier("SEUIL_RUI_PARC","DEFAVORABLE"), RUI_defav(SP)=1;
                  );
                  IF (Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"RUI_PARC") <= R_Tixier("SEUIL_RUI_PARC","DEFAVORABLE") and (Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"RUI_PARC")>R_Tixier("SEUIL_RUI_PARC","FAVORABLE")),
                      RUI_defav(SP)$((R_Tixier("SEUIL_RUI_PARC","DEFAVORABLE") - R_Tixier("SEUIL_RUI_PARC","FAVORABLE")))
                    =((Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"RUI_PARC") - R_Tixier("SEUIL_RUI_PARC","FAVORABLE"))) /((R_Tixier("SEUIL_RUI_PARC","DEFAVORABLE") - R_Tixier("SEUIL_RUI_PARC","FAVORABLE")));
                  );
         );
         STOCK_RUI_defav(SH,SP)= RUI_defav(SP);

         RUI_fav(SP)= 1-RUI_defav(SP);
         STOCK_RUI_fav(SH,SP)= RUI_fav(SP);


         E_SURF(SP,SC)$
                  ( min(DOSE_fav(SC),  RUI_fav(SP))  *ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                    min(DOSE_fav(SC),  RUI_defav(SP))*ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                    min(DOSE_defav(SC),RUI_fav(SP))  *ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                    min(DOSE_defav(SC),RUI_defav(SP) *ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)))
                  =
                  ((0*(min(DOSE_fav(SC),  RUI_fav(SP))  *ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC))+
                   (4*min(DOSE_fav(SC),   RUI_defav(SP))*ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC))+
                   (6*min(DOSE_defav(SC), RUI_fav(SP))  *ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC))+
                   (10*min(DOSE_defav(SC),RUI_defav(SP))*ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC))))
                  /
                  ((min(DOSE_fav(SC),  RUI_fav(SP))  *ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                    min(DOSE_fav(SC),  RUI_defav(SP))*ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                    min(DOSE_defav(SC),RUI_fav(SP))  *ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                    min(DOSE_defav(SC),RUI_defav(SP))*ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)));
         STOCK_E_SURF(SH,SP,SC)= E_SURF(SP,SC);


         LOOP(SC,
                  IF (DT50_Cult(SC) <  R_Tixier("SEUIL_DT50_CULT","FAVORABLE"), DT50_defav(SC) =0;
                  );
                  IF (DT50_Cult(SC) >  R_Tixier("SEUIL_DT50_CULT","DEFAVORABLE"), DT50_defav(SC) =1;
                  );
                  IF (DT50_Cult(SC) <= R_Tixier("SEUIL_DT50_CULT","DEFAVORABLE") and ( DT50_Cult(SC)>R_Tixier("SEUIL_DT50_CULT","FAVORABLE")),
                      DT50_defav(SC)$((R_Tixier("SEUIL_DT50_CULT","DEFAVORABLE") - R_Tixier("SEUIL_DT50_CULT","FAVORABLE")))
                  = ((DT50_Cult(SC) -  R_Tixier("SEUIL_DT50_CULT","FAVORABLE"))) /((R_Tixier("SEUIL_DT50_CULT","DEFAVORABLE") - R_Tixier("SEUIL_DT50_CULT","FAVORABLE")));
                  ;)
         );
         STOCK_DT50_defav(SH,SC)= DT50_defav(SC);

         DT50_fav(SC) = 1-DT50_defav(SC);
         STOCK_DT50_fav(SH,SC)= DT50_fav(SC);


         LOOP(SC,
                  IF ((min(ADI_Cult(SC),AQUATOX_Cult(SC))) >  R_Tixier("SEUIL_MIN_ADIAQUATOX","DEFAVORABLE"), MIN_ADIAQUATOX_defav(SC) =1; );
                  IF ((min(ADI_Cult(SC),AQUATOX_Cult(SC))) <  R_Tixier("SEUIL_MIN_ADIAQUATOX","FAVORABLE"),   MIN_ADIAQUATOX_defav(SC) =0; );
                  IF ((min(ADI_Cult(SC),AQUATOX_Cult(SC))) <= R_Tixier("SEUIL_MIN_ADIAQUATOX","DEFAVORABLE") and
                     ((min(ADI_Cult(SC),AQUATOX_Cult(SC))) >  R_Tixier("SEUIL_MIN_ADIAQUATOX","FAVORABLE")),
                  MIN_ADIAQUATOX_defav(SC)$((R_Tixier("SEUIL_MIN_ADIAQUATOX","DEFAVORABLE") - R_Tixier("SEUIL_MIN_ADIAQUATOX","FAVORABLE")))

                  = ((min(ADI_Cult(SC),AQUATOX_Cult(SC)) - R_Tixier("SEUIL_MIN_ADIAQUATOX","FAVORABLE"))) / ((R_Tixier("SEUIL_MIN_ADIAQUATOX","DEFAVORABLE") - R_Tixier("SEUIL_MIN_ADIAQUATOX","FAVORABLE")));)

*                  = ((DT50_Cult(SC) - R_Tixier("SEUIL_MIN_ADIAQUATOX","FAVORABLE"))) / ((R_Tixier("SEUIL_MIN_ADIAQUATOX","DEFAVORABLE") - R_Tixier("SEUIL_MIN_ADIAQUATOX","FAVORABLE")));)
         );
         STOCK_MIN_ADIAQUATOX_defav(SH,SC)= MIN_ADIAQUATOX_defav(SC);

         MIN_ADIAQUATOX_fav(SC)= 1-MIN_ADIAQUATOX_defav(SC);
         STOCK_MIN_ADIAQUATOX_fav(SH,SC)= MIN_ADIAQUATOX_fav(SC);


         T_SURF(SP,SC)$
                  (min(DT50_fav(SC),  MIN_ADIAQUATOX_fav(SC))  *ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                   min(DT50_fav(SC),  MIN_ADIAQUATOX_defav(SC))*ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                   min(DT50_defav(SC),MIN_ADIAQUATOX_fav(SC))  *ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                   min(DT50_defav(SC),MIN_ADIAQUATOX_defav(SC) *ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)))
                  =
                  ((0*(min(DT50_fav(SC),  MIN_ADIAQUATOX_fav(SC))  *ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC))+
                   (4*min(DT50_fav(SC),   MIN_ADIAQUATOX_defav(SC))*ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC))+
                   (6*min(DT50_defav(SC), MIN_ADIAQUATOX_fav(SC))  *ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC))+
                   (10*min(DT50_defav(SC),MIN_ADIAQUATOX_defav(SC))*ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC))))
                  /
                  ((min(DT50_fav(SC),  MIN_ADIAQUATOX_fav(SC))  *ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                    min(DT50_fav(SC),  MIN_ADIAQUATOX_defav(SC))*ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                    min(DT50_defav(SC),MIN_ADIAQUATOX_fav(SC))  *ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                    min(DT50_defav(SC),MIN_ADIAQUATOX_defav(SC))*ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)));
         STOCK_T_SURF(SH,SP,SC)= T_SURF(SP,SC);


         LOOP(SC,
                  IF ( GUS_Cult(SC) <  R_Tixier("SEUIL_GUS_CULT","FAVORABLE"), GUS_defav(SC) =0;
                  );
                  IF ( GUS_Cult(SC) >  R_Tixier("SEUIL_GUS_CULT","DEFAVORABLE"), GUS_defav(SC) =1;
                  );
                  IF ( GUS_Cult(SC) <= R_Tixier("SEUIL_GUS_CULT","DEFAVORABLE") and ( GUS_Cult(SC)>R_Tixier("SEUIL_GUS_CULT","FAVORABLE")),
                  GUS_defav(SC)$((R_Tixier("SEUIL_GUS_CULT","DEFAVORABLE") - R_Tixier("SEUIL_GUS_CULT","FAVORABLE")))
                  =  ((GUS_Cult(SC) - R_Tixier("SEUIL_GUS_CULT","FAVORABLE"))) /((R_Tixier("SEUIL_GUS_CULT","DEFAVORABLE") - R_Tixier("SEUIL_GUS_CULT","FAVORABLE")));
                  );
         );
         STOCK_GUS_defav(SH,SC)= GUS_defav(SC);

         GUS_fav(SC)= 1-GUS_defav(SC);
         STOCK_GUS_fav(SH,SC)= GUS_fav(SC);





         LOOP(SP,
                  IF ( Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"DRAI_PARC") <  R_Tixier("SEUIL_DRAI_PARC","FAVORABLE"), DRAI_defav(SP) =0; );
                  IF ( Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"DRAI_PARC") >  R_Tixier("SEUIL_DRAI_PARC","DEFAVORABLE"), DRAI_defav(SP) =1; );
                  IF ( Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"DRAI_PARC") <= R_Tixier("SEUIL_DRAI_PARC","DEFAVORABLE") and ( Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"DRAI_PARC")>R_Tixier("SEUIL_DRAI_PARC","FAVORABLE")),
                  DRAI_defav(SP)$((R_Tixier("SEUIL_DRAI_PARC","DEFAVORABLE") - R_Tixier("SEUIL_DRAI_PARC","FAVORABLE")))
                  = ((Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"DRAI_PARC") - R_Tixier("SEUIL_DRAI_PARC","FAVORABLE")))
                  /((R_Tixier("SEUIL_DRAI_PARC","DEFAVORABLE") - R_Tixier("SEUIL_DRAI_PARC","FAVORABLE")));)
         );
         STOCK_DRAI_defav(SH,SP)= DRAI_defav(SP);

         DRAI_fav(SP)= 1-DRAI_defav(SP);
         STOCK_DRAI_fav(SH,SP)= DRAI_fav(SP);


         E_PROF(SP,SC)$
                  ((min(DOSE_fav(SC),  GUS_fav(SC),  DRAI_fav(SP))   *ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                    min(DOSE_fav(SC),  GUS_fav(SC),  DRAI_defav(SP)) *ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                    min(DOSE_fav(SC),  GUS_defav(SC),DRAI_fav(SP))   *ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                    min(DOSE_fav(SC),  GUS_defav(SC),DRAI_defav(SP)) *ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                    min(DOSE_defav(SC),GUS_fav(SC),  DRAI_fav(SP))   *ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                    min(DOSE_defav(SC),GUS_fav(SC),  DRAI_defav(SP)) *ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                    min(DOSE_defav(SC),GUS_defav(SC),DRAI_fav(SP))   *ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                    min(DOSE_defav(SC),GUS_defav(SC),DRAI_defav(SP)))*ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC))
                  =
                  ((0*(min(DOSE_fav(SC),  GUS_fav(SC),  DRAI_fav(SP)))  *ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                   (3*min(DOSE_fav(SC),   GUS_fav(SC),  DRAI_defav(SP)))*ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                   (4*min(DOSE_fav(SC),   GUS_defav(SC),DRAI_fav(SP)))  *ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                   (7*min(DOSE_fav(SC),   GUS_defav(SC),DRAI_defav(SP)))*ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                   (3*min(DOSE_defav(SC), GUS_fav(SC),  DRAI_fav(SP)))  *ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                   (6*min(DOSE_defav(SC), GUS_fav(SC),  DRAI_defav(SP)))*ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                   (7*min(DOSE_defav(SC), GUS_defav(SC),DRAI_fav(SP)))  *ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                   (10*min(DOSE_defav(SC),GUS_defav(SC),DRAI_defav(SP)))*ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)))
                  /
                  ((min(DOSE_fav(SC),  GUS_fav(SC),  DRAI_fav(SP))   *ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                    min(DOSE_fav(SC),  GUS_fav(SC),  DRAI_defav(SP)) *ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                    min(DOSE_fav(SC),  GUS_defav(SC),DRAI_fav(SP))   *ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                    min(DOSE_fav(SC),  GUS_defav(SC),DRAI_defav(SP)) *ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                    min(DOSE_defav(SC),GUS_fav(SC),  DRAI_fav(SP))   *ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                    min(DOSE_defav(SC),GUS_fav(SC),  DRAI_defav(SP)) *ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                    min(DOSE_defav(SC),GUS_defav(SC),DRAI_fav(SP))   *ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                    min(DOSE_defav(SC),GUS_defav(SC),DRAI_defav(SP)))*ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC));
         STOCK_E_PROF(SH,SP,SC)= E_PROF(SP,SC);


         LOOP(SC,
                  IF ( ADI_Cult(SC) <  R_Tixier("SEUIL_ADI_CULT","FAVORABLE"), ADI_defav(SC) =0;
                  );
                  IF ( ADI_Cult(SC) >  R_Tixier("SEUIL_ADI_CULT","DEFAVORABLE"), ADI_defav(SC) =1;
                  );
                  IF ( ADI_Cult(SC) <= R_Tixier("SEUIL_ADI_CULT","DEFAVORABLE") and ( ADI_Cult(SC)>R_Tixier("SEUIL_ADI_CULT","FAVORABLE")),
                  ADI_defav(SC)$((R_Tixier("SEUIL_ADI_CULT","DEFAVORABLE") - R_Tixier("SEUIL_ADI_CULT","FAVORABLE")))
                  = ((ADI_Cult(SC) - R_Tixier("SEUIL_ADI_CULT","FAVORABLE")))
                  /((R_Tixier("SEUIL_ADI_CULT","DEFAVORABLE") - R_Tixier("SEUIL_ADI_CULT","FAVORABLE")));
                  );
         );
         STOCK_ADI_defav(SH,SC)= ADI_defav(SC);

         ADI_fav(SC)= 1-ADI_defav(SC);
         STOCK_ADI_fav(SH,SC)= ADI_fav(SC);


         T_PROF(SP,SC)$
                  ((min(DT50_fav(SC),  ADI_fav(SC))  *ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                    min(DT50_fav(SC),  ADI_defav(SC))*ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                    min(DT50_defav(SC),ADI_fav(SC))  *ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                    min(DT50_defav(SC),ADI_defav(SC))*ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)))
                  =
                  ((0*(min(DT50_fav(SC),  ADI_fav(SC)))  *ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                   (4*min(DT50_fav(SC),   ADI_defav(SC)))*ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                   (6*min(DT50_defav(SC), ADI_fav(SC)))  *ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                   (10*min(DT50_defav(SC),ADI_defav(SC)))*ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)))
                  /
                  ((min(DT50_fav(SC),  ADI_fav(SC))   *ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                    min(DT50_fav(SC),  ADI_defav(SC)) *ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                    min(DT50_defav(SC),ADI_fav(SC))   *ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                    min(DT50_defav(SC),ADI_defav(SC)))*ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC));
         STOCK_T_PROF(SH,SP,SC)= T_PROF(SP,SC);


         R_SURF(SP,SC)= ((E_SURF(SP,SC))+(T_SURF(SP,SC)))/2;
         STOCK_R_SURF(SH,SP,SC)= R_SURF(SP,SC);

         R_PROF(SP,SC)= ((E_PROF(SP,SC))+(T_PROF(SP,SC)))/2;
         STOCK_R_PROF(SH,SP,SC)= R_PROF(SP,SC);


         R_PEST(SP,SC)= max(R_SURF(SP,SC), R_PROF(SP,SC));
         STOCK_R_PEST(SH,SP,SC)= R_PEST(SP,SC);

         R_PEST_parc(SP)$SURF_Parc(SP)= (sum(SC, R_PEST(SP,SC)*ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC))) / SURF_Parc(SP);
         STOCK_R_PEST_Parc(SH,SP)= R_PEST_Parc(SP);

         R_PEST_Gwad =  sum(SP$(R_PEST_parc(SP)>6), SURF_Parc(SP));
         STOCK_R_PEST_Gwad(SH)= R_PEST_Gwad;


****************************************************************************************************************************
*INDICATEUR n°12: Number of rivers potentially polluted  "Enhancing water quality"  oooooooooooooooooooooooooooooooooooooooo
****************************************************************************************************************************

$ontext;
         NV_POLL_RIV(SRIV)$(SURF_RIV(SRIV))= (sum(SP$RIV_Parc(SRIV,SP), (R_PEST_parc(SP)* SURF_PARC(SP))))/SURF_RIV(SRIV);
         STOCK_NV_POLL_RIV(SH,SRIV)= NV_POLL_RIV(SRIV);

         LOOP(SRIV,
                  IF (NV_POLL_RIV(SRIV) <  7, POLL_RIV(SRIV) = 0
                  );
                  IF (NV_POLL_RIV(SRIV) >= 7, POLL_RIV(SRIV) = 1
                  );
         );
         STOCK_POLL_RIV(SH,SRIV)= POLL_RIV(SRIV);

         NB_RIV_POLL = sum(SRIV, POLL_RIV(SRIV));
         STOCK_NB_RIV_POLL(SH)= NB_RIV_POLL;

         LOOP(SRIV,
                  IF(NV_POLL_RIV(SRIV) > -10000 , NB_RIV(SRIV) = 1
                  );
         );
         STOCK_NB_RIV(SH,SRIV)= NB_RIV(SRIV);

         NB_RIV_TOT = sum(SRIV, NB_RIV(SRIV));
         STOCK_NB_RIV_TOT(SH)= NB_RIV_TOT;

         RATIO_RIV_POLL$(NB_RIV_TOT>0) = NB_RIV_POLL / NB_RIV_TOT;
         STOCK_RATIO_RIV_POLL(SH)= RATIO_RIV_POLL;
$offtext;

****************************************************************************************************************************
*INDICATEUR n°13: Number of water catchments potentially polluted  "Enhancing water quality"  oooooooooooooooooooooooooooooo
****************************************************************************************************************************
         NV_POLL_BV(SBV)$(SURF_BV(SBV))= (sum(SP$BV_Parc(SBV,SP), (R_PEST_parc(SP)* SURF_PARC(SP))))/SURF_BV(SBV);
         STOCK_NV_POLL_BV(SH,SBV)= NV_POLL_BV(SBV);

         LOOP(SBV,
                  IF (NV_POLL_BV(SBV) <  7, POLL_BV(SBV) = 0
                  );
                  IF (NV_POLL_BV(SBV) >= 7, POLL_BV(SBV) = 1
                  );
         );
         STOCK_POLL_BV(SH,SBV)= POLL_BV(SBV);

         NB_BV_POLL = sum(SBV, POLL_BV(SBV));
         STOCK_NB_BV_POLL(SH)= NB_BV_POLL;

         LOOP(SBV,
                  IF(NV_POLL_BV(SBV) > -10000 , NB_BV(SBV) = 1
                  );
         );
         STOCK_NB_BV(SH,SBV)= NB_BV(SBV);

         NB_BV_TOT = sum(SBV, NB_BV(SBV));
         STOCK_NB_BV_TOT(SH)= NB_BV_TOT;

         RATIO_BV_POLL$(NB_BV_TOT>0) = NB_BV_POLL / NB_BV_TOT;
         STOCK_RATIO_BV_POLL(SH)= RATIO_BV_POLL;


****************************************************************************************************************************
*INDICATEUR n°14: Number of abstraction water sources potentially polluted  "Enhancing water quality"  ooooooooooooooooooooo
****************************************************************************************************************************
         NV_POLL_CPT(SCPT)$(SURF_CPT(SCPT))= (sum(SP$CPT_Parc(SCPT,SP), (R_PEST_parc(SP)* SURF_PARC(SP))))/SURF_CPT(SCPT);
         STOCK_NV_POLL_CPT(SH,SCPT)= NV_POLL_CPT(SCPT);

         LOOP(SCPT,
                  IF (NV_POLL_CPT(SCPT) <  7 , POLL_CPT(SCPT) = 0
                  );
                  IF (NV_POLL_CPT(SCPT) >= 7 , POLL_CPT(SCPT) = 1
                  );
         );
         STOCK_POLL_CPT(SH,SCPT)= POLL_CPT(SCPT);

         NB_CPT_POLL = sum(SCPT, POLL_CPT(SCPT));
         STOCK_NB_CPT_POLL(SH)= NB_CPT_POLL;

         LOOP(SCPT,
                  IF(NV_POLL_CPT(SCPT) > -10000 , NB_CPT(SCPT) = 1
                  );
         );
         STOCK_NB_CPT(SH,SCPT)= NB_CPT(SCPT);

         NB_CPT_TOT = sum(SCPT, NB_CPT(SCPT));
         STOCK_NB_CPT_TOT(SH)= NB_CPT_TOT;

         RATIO_CPT_POLL$(NB_CPT_TOT>0) = NB_CPT_POLL / NB_CPT_TOT;
         STOCK_RATIO_CPT_POLL(SH)= RATIO_CPT_POLL;


****************************************************************************************************************************
*INDICATEUR n°15: Amount of water used for irrigation  "Enhancing water quality"  oooooooooooooooooooooooooooooooooooooooooo
****************************************************************************************************************************
         BESOIN_EAU_01_parc(SP) = sum (SC, (Data_cult("BESOIN_EAU_01",SC) * ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)))  ;
         STOCK_BESOIN_EAU_01_parc(SH,SP) = BESOIN_EAU_01_parc(SP);

         BESOIN_EAU_02_parc(SP) = sum (SC, (Data_cult("BESOIN_EAU_02",SC) * ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)))  ;
         STOCK_BESOIN_EAU_02_parc(SH,SP) = BESOIN_EAU_02_parc(SP);

         BESOIN_EAU_03_parc(SP) = sum (SC, (Data_cult("BESOIN_EAU_03",SC) * ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)))  ;
         STOCK_BESOIN_EAU_03_parc(SH,SP) = BESOIN_EAU_03_parc(SP);

         BESOIN_EAU_04_parc(SP) = sum (SC, (Data_cult("BESOIN_EAU_04",SC) * ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)))  ;
         STOCK_BESOIN_EAU_04_parc(SH,SP) = BESOIN_EAU_04_parc(SP);

         BESOIN_EAU_05_parc(SP) = sum (SC, (Data_cult("BESOIN_EAU_05",SC) * ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)))  ;
         STOCK_BESOIN_EAU_05_parc(SH,SP) = BESOIN_EAU_05_parc(SP);

         BESOIN_EAU_06_parc(SP) = sum (SC, (Data_cult("BESOIN_EAU_06",SC) * ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)))  ;
         STOCK_BESOIN_EAU_06_parc(SH,SP) = BESOIN_EAU_06_parc(SP);

         BESOIN_EAU_07_parc(SP) = sum (SC, (Data_cult("BESOIN_EAU_07",SC) * ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)))  ;
         STOCK_BESOIN_EAU_07_parc(SH,SP) = BESOIN_EAU_07_parc(SP);

         BESOIN_EAU_08_parc(SP) = sum (SC, (Data_cult("BESOIN_EAU_08",SC) * ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)))  ;
         STOCK_BESOIN_EAU_08_parc(SH,SP) = BESOIN_EAU_08_parc(SP);

         BESOIN_EAU_09_parc(SP) = sum (SC, (Data_cult("BESOIN_EAU_09",SC) * ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)))  ;
         STOCK_BESOIN_EAU_09_parc(SH,SP) = BESOIN_EAU_09_parc(SP);

         BESOIN_EAU_10_parc(SP) = sum (SC, (Data_cult("BESOIN_EAU_10",SC) * ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)))  ;
         STOCK_BESOIN_EAU_10_parc(SH,SP) = BESOIN_EAU_10_parc(SP);

         BESOIN_EAU_11_parc(SP) = sum (SC, (Data_cult("BESOIN_EAU_11",SC) * ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)))  ;
         STOCK_BESOIN_EAU_11_parc(SH,SP) = BESOIN_EAU_11_parc(SP);

         BESOIN_EAU_12_parc(SP) = sum (SC, (Data_cult("BESOIN_EAU_12",SC) * ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)))  ;
         STOCK_BESOIN_EAU_12_parc(SH,SP) = BESOIN_EAU_12_parc(SP);


         LOOP (SP,
                  IF (Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"IRRIG_PARC") = 1,
                          IRRIG_PARC(SP) = BESOIN_EAU_01_parc(SP) -  Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"PLUVIO_01_PARC")$(BESOIN_EAU_01_parc(SP)>Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"PLUVIO_01_PARC")) +
                                           BESOIN_EAU_02_parc(SP) -  Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"PLUVIO_02_PARC")$(BESOIN_EAU_02_parc(SP)>Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"PLUVIO_02_PARC")) +
                                           BESOIN_EAU_03_parc(SP) -  Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"PLUVIO_03_PARC")$(BESOIN_EAU_03_parc(SP)>Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"PLUVIO_03_PARC")) +
                                           BESOIN_EAU_04_parc(SP) -  Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"PLUVIO_04_PARC")$(BESOIN_EAU_04_parc(SP)>Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"PLUVIO_04_PARC")) +
                                           BESOIN_EAU_05_parc(SP) -  Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"PLUVIO_05_PARC")$(BESOIN_EAU_05_parc(SP)>Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"PLUVIO_05_PARC")) +
                                           BESOIN_EAU_06_parc(SP) -  Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"PLUVIO_06_PARC")$(BESOIN_EAU_06_parc(SP)>Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"PLUVIO_06_PARC")) +
                                           BESOIN_EAU_07_parc(SP) -  Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"PLUVIO_07_PARC")$(BESOIN_EAU_07_parc(SP)>Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"PLUVIO_07_PARC")) +
                                           BESOIN_EAU_08_parc(SP) -  Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"PLUVIO_08_PARC")$(BESOIN_EAU_08_parc(SP)>Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"PLUVIO_08_PARC")) +
                                           BESOIN_EAU_09_parc(SP) -  Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"PLUVIO_09_PARC")$(BESOIN_EAU_09_parc(SP)>Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"PLUVIO_09_PARC")) +
                                           BESOIN_EAU_10_parc(SP) -  Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"PLUVIO_10_PARC")$(BESOIN_EAU_10_parc(SP)>Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"PLUVIO_10_PARC")) +
                                           BESOIN_EAU_11_parc(SP) -  Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"PLUVIO_11_PARC")$(BESOIN_EAU_11_parc(SP)>Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"PLUVIO_11_PARC")) +
                                           BESOIN_EAU_12_parc(SP) -  Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"PLUVIO_12_PARC")$(BESOIN_EAU_12_parc(SP)>Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"PLUVIO_12_PARC"))
                  );
                  IF (Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"IRRIG_PARC") = 0,
                          IRRIG_PARC(SP) = 0
                  );
         );
         STOCK_IRRIG_PARC(SH,SP) = IRRIG_PARC(SP);


         LOOP (SP,
                  IF (IRRIG_PARC(SP)<=0, IRRIG_PARC2(SP)= 0
                  );
                  IF (IRRIG_PARC(SP)> 0, IRRIG_PARC2(SP)= IRRIG_PARC(SP)
                  );
         );
         STOCK_IRRIG_PARC2(SH,SP) = IRRIG_PARC2(SP);

         EAU_EXPL(SE) = sum(SP$Expl_Parc(SE,SP), IRRIG_PARC2(SP));
         STOCK_EAU_EXPL(SH,SE) = EAU_EXPL(SE);

         BESOIN_EAU_Gwad = ( sum(SP, IRRIG_PARC2(SP)))/ 1000000;
         STOCK_BESOIN_EAU_Gwad(SH) = BESOIN_EAU_Gwad;


****************************************************************************************************************************
*INDICATEUR n°16: Percentage of the area potentially eroded due to farming practices R_ERO de Tixier
****************************************************************************************************************************

         LOOP(SP,
                  IF ( Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"SENS_W_ECOU") >= R_Tixier("SEUIL_SENS_W_ECOU","FAVORABLE"),   SENS_W_ECOU_defav(SP) =0; );
                  IF ( Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"SENS_W_ECOU") <= R_Tixier("SEUIL_SENS_W_ECOU","DEFAVORABLE"), SENS_W_ECOU_defav(SP) =1; );
                  IF ( Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"SENS_W_ECOU") >  R_Tixier("SEUIL_SENS_W_ECOU","DEFAVORABLE") and ( Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"SENS_W_ECOU") < R_Tixier("SEUIL_SENS_W_ECOU","FAVORABLE")) ,
                          SENS_W_ECOU_defav(SP)= (1 /(1+(EXP((-9+(Data_RPG_Gwad(SP,"SENS_W_ECOU")*0.2))))));
                  )
         );
         STOCK_SENS_W_ECOU_defav(SH,SP)= SENS_W_ECOU_defav(SP);

         SENS_W_ECOU_fav(SP)= 1- SENS_W_ECOU_defav(SP);
         STOCK_SENS_W_ECOU_fav(SH,SP)= SENS_W_ECOU_fav(SP);


         LOOP(SP,
                  IF ( Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"PLUVIO_PARC") <= R_Tixier("SEUIL_PLUIE","FAVORABLE"),   PLUIE_defav(SP) =0; );
                  IF ( Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"PLUVIO_PARC") >= R_Tixier("SEUIL_PLUIE","DEFAVORABLE"), PLUIE_defav(SP) =1; );
                  IF ( Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"PLUVIO_PARC") <  R_Tixier("SEUIL_PLUIE","DEFAVORABLE") and ( Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"PLUVIO_PARC") > R_Tixier("SEUIL_PLUIE","FAVORABLE")) ,
                           PLUIE_defav(SP)$((R_Tixier("SEUIL_PLUIE","FAVORABLE") - R_Tixier("SEUIL_PLUIE","DEFAVORABLE")))
                           = ((Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"PLUVIO_PARC")) - R_Tixier("SEUIL_PLUIE","FAVORABLE"))
                           /(( R_Tixier("SEUIL_PLUIE","DEFAVORABLE")- R_Tixier("SEUIL_PLUIE","FAVORABLE")));
                  )
         );
         STOCK_PLUIE_defav(SH,SP)= PLUIE_defav(SP);

         PLUIE_fav(SP) = 1 - PLUIE_defav(SP);
         STOCK_PLUIE_fav(SH,SP)= PLUIE_fav(SP);


         LOOP(SP,
                  IF ( Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"PENTE") <= R_Tixier("SEUIL_PENTE","FAVORABLE"),   PENTE_defav(SP) =0; );
                  IF ( Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"PENTE") >= R_Tixier("SEUIL_PENTE","DEFAVORABLE"), PENTE_defav(SP) =1; );
                  IF ( Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"PENTE") <  R_Tixier("SEUIL_PENTE","DEFAVORABLE") and ( Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"PENTE") > R_Tixier("SEUIL_PENTE","FAVORABLE")) ,
                  PENTE_defav(SP)$((R_Tixier("SEUIL_PENTE","FAVORABLE") - R_Tixier("SEUIL_PENTE","DEFAVORABLE")))
                  =  ((Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"PENTE")) - R_Tixier("SEUIL_PENTE","FAVORABLE"))
                  / (( R_Tixier("SEUIL_PENTE","DEFAVORABLE")- R_Tixier("SEUIL_PENTE","FAVORABLE")));)
         );
         STOCK_PENTE_defav(SH,SP)= PENTE_defav(SP);

         PENTE_fav(SP) = 1 - PENTE_defav(SP);
         STOCK_PENTE_fav(SH,SP)= PENTE_fav(SP);


         LOOP(SC,
                  IF ( Data_Cult("COUV_SOL",SC) >  R_Tixier("SEUIL_COUV_SOL","FAVORABLE"),   COUV_SOL_defav(SC) =0; );
                  IF ( Data_Cult("COUV_SOL",SC) <  R_Tixier("SEUIL_COUV_SOL","DEFAVORABLE"), COUV_SOL_defav(SC) =1; );
                  IF ( Data_Cult("COUV_SOL",SC) <= R_Tixier("SEUIL_COUV_SOL","FAVORABLE") and ( Data_Cult("COUV_SOL",SC)> R_Tixier("SEUIL_COUV_SOL","DEFAVORABLE")),
                          COUV_SOL_defav(SC)$((R_Tixier("SEUIL_COUV_SOL","DEFAVORABLE") - R_Tixier("SEUIL_COUV_SOL","FAVORABLE")))
                          = ((R_Tixier("SEUIL_COUV_SOL","FAVORABLE") - Data_Cult("COUV_SOL",SC) ))
                          /((R_Tixier("SEUIL_COUV_SOL","FAVORABLE") -R_Tixier("SEUIL_COUV_SOL","DEFAVORABLE")));
                  )
         );
         STOCK_COUV_SOL_defav(SH,SC)= COUV_SOL_defav(SC);

         COUV_SOL_fav(SC)= 1- COUV_SOL_defav(SC);
         STOCK_COUV_SOL_fav(SH,SC)= COUV_SOL_fav(SC);


         LOOP(SC,
                  IF ( Data_Cult("PROF_SILLONS",SC) <=  R_Tixier("SEUIL_PROF_SILLONS","FAVORABLE"),   PROF_SILLONS_defav(SC) =0; );
                  IF ( Data_Cult("PROF_SILLONS",SC) >=  R_Tixier("SEUIL_PROF_SILLONS","DEFAVORABLE"), PROF_SILLONS_defav(SC) =1; );
                  IF ( Data_Cult("PROF_SILLONS",SC) < R_Tixier("SEUIL_PROF_SILLONS","DEFAVORABLE") and ( Data_Cult("PROF_SILLONS",SC)> R_Tixier("SEUIL_PROF_SILLONS","FAVORABLE")),
                           PROF_SILLONS_defav(SC)$((R_Tixier("SEUIL_PROF_SILLONS","DEFAVORABLE") - R_Tixier("SEUIL_PROF_SILLONS","FAVORABLE")))
                           = ((Data_Cult("PROF_SILLONS",SC) - R_Tixier("SEUIL_PROF_SILLONS","FAVORABLE")))
                           /((R_Tixier("SEUIL_PROF_SILLONS","DEFAVORABLE") - R_Tixier("SEUIL_PROF_SILLONS","FAVORABLE")));
                  )
         );
         STOCK_PROF_SILLONS_defav(SH,SC)= PROF_SILLONS_defav(SC);

         PROF_SILLONS_fav(SC)= 1- PROF_SILLONS_defav(SC);
         STOCK_PROF_SILLONS_fav(SH,SC)= PROF_SILLONS_fav(SC);


******** RRP *******************************************************************
         RRP(SP,SC)$(    min(COUV_SOL_fav(SC),   SENS_W_ECOU_fav(SP)  , PROF_SILLONS_fav(SC))   *ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                         min(COUV_SOL_fav(SC),   SENS_W_ECOU_fav(SP)  , PROF_SILLONS_defav(SC)) *ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                         min(COUV_SOL_fav(SC),   SENS_W_ECOU_defav(SP), PROF_SILLONS_fav(SC))   *ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                         min(COUV_SOL_fav(SC),   SENS_W_ECOU_defav(SP), PROF_SILLONS_defav(SC)) *ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                         min(COUV_SOL_defav(SC), SENS_W_ECOU_fav(SP))                           *ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                         min(COUV_SOL_defav(SC), SENS_W_ECOU_defav(SP))                         *ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC))
                  =
                   (0  * min(COUV_SOL_fav(SC),   SENS_W_ECOU_fav(SP),   PROF_SILLONS_fav(SC))  *ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                    2  * min(COUV_SOL_fav(SC),   SENS_W_ECOU_fav(SP),   PROF_SILLONS_defav(SC))*ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                    3  * min(COUV_SOL_fav(SC),   SENS_W_ECOU_defav(SP), PROF_SILLONS_fav(SC))  *ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                    6  * min(COUV_SOL_fav(SC),   SENS_W_ECOU_defav(SP), PROF_SILLONS_defav(SC))*ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                    7  * min(COUV_SOL_defav(SC), SENS_W_ECOU_fav(SP))                          *ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                    10 * min(COUV_SOL_defav(SC), SENS_W_ECOU_defav(SP))                        *ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC))
                  /
                   (     min(COUV_SOL_fav(SC),   SENS_W_ECOU_fav(SP),   PROF_SILLONS_fav(SC))   *ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                         min(COUV_SOL_fav(SC),   SENS_W_ECOU_fav(SP),   PROF_SILLONS_defav(SC)) *ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                         min(COUV_SOL_fav(SC),   SENS_W_ECOU_defav(SP), PROF_SILLONS_fav(SC))   *ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                         min(COUV_SOL_fav(SC),   SENS_W_ECOU_defav(SP), PROF_SILLONS_defav(SC)) *ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                         min(COUV_SOL_defav(SC), SENS_W_ECOU_fav(SP))                           *ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                         min(COUV_SOL_defav(SC), SENS_W_ECOU_defav(SP))                         *ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC));
         STOCK_RRP(SH,SP,SC)= RRP(SP,SC);

         RRP_Parc(SP)$SURF_Parc(SP)= (sum(SC, (RRP(SP,SC) * ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)))) /SURF_Parc(SP);
         STOCK_RRP_Parc(SH,SP)= RRP_Parc(SP);

         LOOP(SP,
                  IF ( RRP_parc(SP) <  R_Tixier("SEUIL_RRP","FAVORABLE"),   RRP_defav(SP) =0; );
                  IF ( RRP_parc(SP) >  R_Tixier("SEUIL_RRP","DEFAVORABLE"), RRP_defav(SP) =1; );
                  IF ( RRP_parc(SP) <= R_Tixier("SEUIL_RRP","DEFAVORABLE") and (RRP_parc(SP) > R_Tixier("SEUIL_RRP","FAVORABLE")),
                       RRP_defav(SP)$((R_Tixier("SEUIL_RRP","DEFAVORABLE") - R_Tixier("SEUIL_RRP","FAVORABLE")))
                    = (RRP_parc(SP) -  R_Tixier("SEUIL_RRP","FAVORABLE"))
                  / ((R_Tixier("SEUIL_RRP","DEFAVORABLE") - R_Tixier("SEUIL_RRP","FAVORABLE")));)
         );
         STOCK_RRP_defav(SH,SP)= RRP_defav(SP);

         RRP_fav(SP) = 1 - RRP_defav(SP);
         STOCK_RRP_fav(SH,SP)= RRP_fav(SP);


******** RRR *******************************************************************


         RRR(SP,SC)$(    min(RRP_fav(SP),PLUIE_fav(SP),  PENTE_fav(SP))  * ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                         min(RRP_fav(SP),PLUIE_fav(SP),  PENTE_defav(SP))* ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                         min(RRP_fav(SP),PLUIE_defav(SP),PENTE_fav(SP))  * ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                         min(RRP_fav(SP),PLUIE_defav(SP),PENTE_defav(SP))* ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                         RRP_defav(SP)                                   * ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC))
                  =
                   (0  * min(RRP_fav(SP),PLUIE_fav(SP),  PENTE_fav(SP))   * ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                    2  * min(RRP_fav(SP),PLUIE_fav(SP),  PENTE_defav(SP)) * ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                    3  * min(RRP_fav(SP),PLUIE_defav(SP),PENTE_fav(SP))   * ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                    6  * min(RRP_fav(SP),PLUIE_defav(SP),PENTE_defav(SP)) * ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                    10 * RRP_defav(SP)                                    * ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC))
                  /
                   (     min(RRP_fav(SP),PLUIE_fav(SP),  PENTE_fav(SP))  * ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                         min(RRP_fav(SP),PLUIE_fav(SP),  PENTE_defav(SP))* ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                         min(RRP_fav(SP),PLUIE_defav(SP),PENTE_fav(SP))  * ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                         min(RRP_fav(SP),PLUIE_defav(SP),PENTE_defav(SP))* ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                        (RRP_defav(SP)                                  * ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)));
         STOCK_RRR(SH,SP,SC)= RRR(SP,SC);

         RRR_parc(SP)$SURF_Parc(SP)= (sum(SC, (RRP(SP,SC)*X.l(SP,SC)))) /SURF_Parc(SP);
         STOCK_RRR_Parc(SH,SP)= RRR_Parc(SP);

         LOOP(SP,
                  IF ( RRR_parc(SP) <= R_Tixier("SEUIL_RRR","FAVORABLE"),   RRR_defav(SP) =0; );
                  IF ( RRR_parc(SP) >= R_Tixier("SEUIL_RRR","DEFAVORABLE"), RRR_defav(SP) =1; );
                  IF ( RRR_parc(SP) <  R_Tixier("SEUIL_RRR","DEFAVORABLE") and (RRR_parc(SP) > R_Tixier("SEUIL_RRR","FAVORABLE")),
                       RRR_defav(SP)$((R_Tixier("SEUIL_RRR","DEFAVORABLE") - R_Tixier("SEUIL_RRR","FAVORABLE")))
                    = (RRR_parc(SP) - R_Tixier("SEUIL_RRR","FAVORABLE"))
                  / ((R_Tixier("SEUIL_RRR","DEFAVORABLE") - R_Tixier("SEUIL_RRR","FAVORABLE")));)
         );
         STOCK_RRR_defav(SH,SP)= RRR_defav(SP);

         RRR_fav(SP) = 1 - RRR_defav(SP);
         STOCK_RRR_fav(SH,SP)= RRR_fav(SP);


******** R_ERO *****************************************************************
         R_ERO(SP,SC)$(  min(COUV_SOL_fav(SC), RRR_defav(SP))* ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                         min(COUV_SOL_fav(SC), RRR_fav(SP))  * ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                         COUV_SOL_defav(SC)                  * ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC))
                  =
                   (1.5* min(COUV_SOL_fav(SC), RRR_defav(SP))* ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                    6  * min(COUV_SOL_fav(SC), RRR_fav(SP))  * ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)+
                    10 * COUV_SOL_defav(SC)                  * ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC))
                  /
                   (     min(COUV_SOL_fav(SC), RRR_defav(SP))* ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC) +
                         min(COUV_SOL_fav(SC), RRR_fav(SP))  * ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC) +
                         COUV_SOL_defav(SC)                  * ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC));
         STOCK_R_ERO(SH,SP,SC)= R_ERO(SP,SC);

         R_ERO_Parc(SP)$SURF_Parc_init(SP) = sum(SC,((R_ERO(SP,SC)* X.l(SP,SC)) / (SURF_PARC_init(SP))));
         STOCK_R_ERO_Parc(SH,SP)= R_ERO_Parc(SP);


         LOOP(SP,
* si vertisol ou autre,
                  IF(   Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"TYPE_SOL")=1 OR Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"TYPE_SOL")=5,
                     R_ERO_parc2(SP)= 0.1*R_ERO_parc(SP);
                  );
* si Sol ferralitique ou Andosol ou Nitisol,
                  IF(   Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"TYPE_SOL")=2 OR Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"TYPE_SOL")=3 OR Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"TYPE_SOL")=4,
                     R_ERO_parc2(SP)= 0.7*R_ERO_parc(SP);
                  );
         );
         STOCK_R_ERO_Parc2(SH,SP)= R_ERO_Parc2(SP);

         LOOP(SP,
                  IF (R_ERO_parc2(SP)> 10, R_ERO_parc3(SP)=10;
                  );
                  IF (R_ERO_parc2(SP)<=10, R_ERO_parc3(SP)=R_ERO_parc2(SP);
                  );
         );
         STOCK_R_ERO_Parc3(SH,SP)= R_ERO_Parc3(SP);

         R_ERO_Gwad = sum(SP$(R_ERO_Parc3(SP)>6), SURF_Parc(SP));
         STOCK_R_ERO_Gwad(SH)= R_ERO_Gwad;


****************************************************************************************************************************
*INDICATEUR n°17: Overall emissions of CO2 from farming activities "Decreasing the contribution to climate change"  oooooooo
****************************************************************************************************************************
         GES_Parc(SP)          = sum(SC,ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)*GES_Ha_Cult(SC) );
         STOCK_GES_Parc(SH,SP) = GES_Parc(SP);

         GES_Cult(SC)          = sum(SP,ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)*GES_Ha_Cult(SC));
         STOCK_GES_Cult(SH,SC) = GES_Cult(SC);

         GES_Expl(SE)          = sum(SP$Expl_Parc(SE,SP), GES_Parc(SP));
         STOCK_GES_Expl(SH,SE) = GES_Expl(SE);

         GES_Reg (SR)          = sum(SP$Reg_Parc(SR,SP),  GES_Parc(SP));
         STOCK_GES_Reg (SH,SR) = GES_Reg (SR);

         GES_Gwad              = sum(SP,                  GES_Parc(SP));
         STOCK_GES_Gwad(SH)    = GES_Gwad;

         GES_Gwad_Moy_ha       = GES_Gwad / ASSOL_GWAD_tot_TOTAL;

****************************************************************************************************************************
*INDICATEUR n°18: Diversity of crops across landscape "Improving the diversity of agricultural landscapes"  oooooooooooooooo
****************************************************************************************************************************
*ooooooo Calcul de l'Inverse Simpson Index (Crop diversity dans MASC) - indicateur de diversification des systèmes de cultures dans l'assolement

         ISI_SOBT_PLANT$SURF_SOBT_tot = 1/((sqr(ASSOL_SOBT_tot_CAN / SURF_SOBT_tot)) + (sqr(ASSOL_SOBT_tot_BAN / SURF_SOBT_tot))
                           + (sqr(ASSOL_SOBT_tot_PAT / SURF_SOBT_tot)) + (sqr(ASSOL_SOBT_tot_NON / SURF_SOBT_tot))
                           + (sqr(ASSOL_SOBT_tot_ANA / SURF_SOBT_tot)) + (sqr(ASSOL_SOBT_tot_PLU / SURF_SOBT_tot))
                           + (sqr(ASSOL_SOBT_tot_MAR / SURF_SOBT_tot)));
         STOCK_ISI_SOBT_PLANT(SH) = ISI_SOBT_PLANT;

         ISI_SEBT_PLANT$SURF_SEBT_tot = 1/((sqr(ASSOL_SEBT_tot_CAN / SURF_SEBT_tot)) + (sqr(ASSOL_SEBT_tot_BAN / SURF_SEBT_tot))
                           + (sqr(ASSOL_SEBT_tot_PAT / SURF_SEBT_tot)) + (sqr(ASSOL_SEBT_tot_NON / SURF_SEBT_tot))
                           + (sqr(ASSOL_SEBT_tot_ANA / SURF_SEBT_tot)) + (sqr(ASSOL_SEBT_tot_PLU / SURF_SEBT_tot))
                           + (sqr(ASSOL_SEBT_tot_MAR / SURF_SEBT_tot)));
         STOCK_ISI_SEBT_PLANT(SH) = ISI_SEBT_PLANT;

         ISI_NBT_PLANT$SURF_NBT_tot  = 1/((sqr(ASSOL_NBT_tot_CAN  / SURF_NBT_tot )) + (sqr(ASSOL_NBT_tot_BAN  / SURF_NBT_tot))
                           + (sqr(ASSOL_NBT_tot_PAT  / SURF_NBT_tot )) + (sqr(ASSOL_NBT_tot_NON  / SURF_NBT_tot))
                           + (sqr(ASSOL_NBT_tot_ANA  / SURF_NBT_tot )) + (sqr(ASSOL_NBT_tot_PLU  / SURF_NBT_tot))
                           + (sqr(ASSOL_NBT_tot_MAR  / SURF_NBT_tot )));
         STOCK_ISI_NBT_PLANT(SH)  = ISI_NBT_PLANT;

         ISI_NGT_PLANT$SURF_NGT_tot  = 1/((sqr(ASSOL_NGT_tot_CAN  / SURF_NGT_tot )) + (sqr(ASSOL_NGT_tot_BAN  / SURF_NGT_tot))
                           + (sqr(ASSOL_NGT_tot_PAT  / SURF_NGT_tot )) + (sqr(ASSOL_NGT_tot_NON  / SURF_NGT_tot))
                           + (sqr(ASSOL_NGT_tot_ANA  / SURF_NGT_tot )) + (sqr(ASSOL_NGT_tot_PLU  / SURF_NGT_tot))
                           + (sqr(ASSOL_NGT_tot_MAR  / SURF_NGT_tot )));
         STOCK_ISI_NGT_PLANT(SH)  = ISI_NGT_PLANT;

         ISI_CGT_PLANT$SURF_CGT_tot  = 1/((sqr(ASSOL_CGT_tot_CAN  / SURF_CGT_tot )) + (sqr(ASSOL_CGT_tot_BAN  / SURF_CGT_tot))
                           + (sqr(ASSOL_CGT_tot_PAT  / SURF_CGT_tot )) + (sqr(ASSOL_CGT_tot_NON  / SURF_CGT_tot))
                           + (sqr(ASSOL_CGT_tot_ANA  / SURF_CGT_tot )) + (sqr(ASSOL_CGT_tot_PLU  / SURF_CGT_tot))
                           + (sqr(ASSOL_CGT_tot_MAR  / SURF_CGT_tot )));
         STOCK_ISI_CGT_PLANT(SH)  = ISI_CGT_PLANT;

         ISI_EGT_PLANT$SURF_EGT_tot  = 1/((sqr(ASSOL_EGT_tot_CAN  / SURF_EGT_tot )) + (sqr(ASSOL_EGT_tot_BAN  / SURF_EGT_tot))
                           + (sqr(ASSOL_EGT_tot_PAT  / SURF_EGT_tot )) + (sqr(ASSOL_EGT_tot_NON  / SURF_EGT_tot))
                           + (sqr(ASSOL_EGT_tot_ANA  / SURF_EGT_tot )) + (sqr(ASSOL_EGT_tot_PLU  / SURF_EGT_tot))
                           + (sqr(ASSOL_EGT_tot_MAR  / SURF_EGT_tot )));
         STOCK_ISI_EGT_PLANT(SH)  = ISI_EGT_PLANT;

         ISI_MG_PLANT$SURF_MG_tot   = 1/((sqr(ASSOL_MG_tot_CAN   / SURF_MG_tot  )) + (sqr(ASSOL_MG_tot_BAN   / SURF_MG_tot))
                           + (sqr(ASSOL_MG_tot_PAT   / SURF_MG_tot  )) + (sqr(ASSOL_MG_tot_NON   / SURF_MG_tot))
                           + (sqr(ASSOL_MG_tot_ANA   / SURF_MG_tot  )) + (sqr(ASSOL_MG_tot_PLU   / SURF_MG_tot))
                           + (sqr(ASSOL_MG_tot_MAR   / SURF_MG_tot  )));
         STOCK_ISI_MG_PLANT(SH)   = ISI_MG_PLANT;

         ISI_TOT_PLANT = (ISI_SOBT_PLANT + ISI_SEBT_PLANT + ISI_NBT_PLANT + ISI_NGT_PLANT + ISI_CGT_PLANT + ISI_EGT_PLANT + ISI_MG_PLANT)/7;
         STOCK_ISI_TOT_PLANT(SH)  = ISI_TOT_PLANT;


************************************************************************************************************************
*Declaration autres INDICATEURS: IFT - AZOTE  - RFERT - THEIL - SURFACE_BIO - ISI_cropping systems - IC-PHYTO oooooooooo
************************************************************************************************************************

*IFT*******************
         IFT_Parc(SP)          = sum(SC,ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)*IFT_Ha_Cult(SC));
         STOCK_IFT_Parc(SH,SP) = IFT_Parc(SP);

         IFT_Cult(SC)          = sum(SP,ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)*IFT_Ha_Cult(SC));
         STOCK_IFT_Cult(SH,SC) = IFT_Cult(SC);

         IFT_Expl(SE)          = sum(SP$Expl_Parc(SE,SP), IFT_Parc(SP));
         STOCK_IFT_Expl(SH,SE) = IFT_Expl(SE);

         IFT_Reg(SR)           = sum(SP$Reg_Parc(SR,SP),  IFT_Parc(SP));
         STOCK_IFT_Reg(SH,SR)  = IFT_Reg(SR);

         IFT_Gwad              = sum(SP,IFT_Parc(SP));
         STOCK_IFT_Gwad(SH)    = IFT_Gwad;

         IFT_Moy_ha_Gwad       = IFT_Gwad / ASSOL_GWAD_tot_TOTAL;

*ooooooo Calcul des quantités d'azote apportées aux différentes echelles (en kg d'azote)
         AZOTE_Parc(SP)          = sum(SC,ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)*AZOTE_Ha_Cult(SC) );
         STOCK_AZOTE_Parc(SH,SP) = AZOTE_Parc(SP);

         AZOTE_Cult(SC)          = sum(SP,ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)*AZOTE_Ha_Cult(SC));
         STOCK_AZOTE_Cult(SH,SC) = AZOTE_Cult(SC);

         AZOTE_Expl(SE)          = sum(SP$Expl_Parc(SE,SP), AZOTE_Parc(SP) );
         STOCK_AZOTE_Expl(SH,SE) = AZOTE_Expl(SE);

         AZOTE_Reg(SR)           = sum(SP$Reg_Parc(SR,SP),  AZOTE_Parc(SP) );
         STOCK_AZOTE_Reg(SH,SR)  = AZOTE_Reg(SR);

         AZOTE_Gwad              = sum(SP,                  AZOTE_Parc(SP) );
         STOCK_AZOTE_Gwad(SH)    = AZOTE_Gwad;

         AZOTE_Moy_ha_Gwad       = AZOTE_Gwad / ASSOL_GWAD_tot_TOTAL;


*MMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMM
*MMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMM MORGWANIK MMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMM
*MMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMM


*XXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXX ENTREES DE CARBONE PAR ETAT INITIAL XXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXX

*ooooooo calcul de la quantité de carbone initiale par hectare en fonction de la teneur en Carbone du sol (en %) et de la nature du sol
         LOOP(SP,
                  IF (Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"TYPE_SOL")=1,
                          C_ORG_ha_Parc_init(SP) =  Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"PART_C_INIT")/100 * Data_Sol("DENS","VERTISOL") * Data_Sol("PROF","VERTISOL") * 10000;
                  );
                  IF (Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"TYPE_SOL")=2,
                 C_ORG_ha_Parc_init(SP) =  Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"PART_C_INIT")/100 * Data_Sol("DENS","FERRALSOL") * Data_Sol("PROF","FERRALSOL") * 10000;
                  );
                  IF (Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"TYPE_SOL")=3,
                          C_ORG_ha_Parc_init(SP) =  Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"PART_C_INIT")/100 * Data_Sol("DENS","ANDOSOL") * Data_Sol("PROF","ANDOSOL") * 10000;
                  );
                  IF (Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"TYPE_SOL")=4,
                          C_ORG_ha_Parc_init(SP) =  Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"PART_C_INIT")/100 * Data_Sol("DENS","NITISOL") * Data_Sol("PROF","NITISOL") * 10000;
                  );
                  IF (Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"TYPE_SOL")=5,
                          C_ORG_ha_Parc_init(SP) =  Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"PART_C_INIT")/100 * Data_Sol("DENS","AUTRES") * Data_Sol("PROF","AUTRES") * 10000;
                  );
         );
         STOCK_C_ORG_ha_Parc_init(SH,SP)= C_ORG_ha_Parc_init(SP);
*ooooooo calcul de la quantité de carbone initiale par parcelle (prise en compte de la surface de la parcelle) (en tonnes par parcelle)
         C_ORG_Parc_init(SP) = C_ORG_ha_Parc_init(SP)*Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"SURF_HA");
         STOCK_C_ORG_Parc_init(SH,SP)= C_ORG_Parc_init(SP);

         IF( NB_BOUCLE<=2 ,
                  C_ORG_ha_Parc(SP)= C_ORG_ha_Parc_init(SP);
                  C_ORG_Parc(SP)= C_ORG_ha_Parc(SP)*Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"SURF_HA");

         ELSE
*XXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXX ENTREES DE CARBONE PAR LA CULTURE XXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXX
*ooooooo Calcul de la biomasse aerienne des cultures pour MORGWANIK oooooooooooo
         BIOM_AER_Cult(SC)= Data_cult("BIOM_AER",SC);
*ooooooo calcul de la biomasse racinaire ooooooooooooooooooooooooooooooooooooooo
         BIOM_RAC_Cult(SC)= BIOM_AER_Cult(SC) * Data_Cult("RAC",SC);
*ooooooo calcul de la quantité de résidus oooooooooooooooooooooooooooooooooooooo
         Q_RES_Cult(SC)= BIOM_AER_Cult(SC) + BIOM_RAC_Cult(SC);
*ooooooo calcul de la quantité de carbone apportée par les cultures à la parcelle (en tonnes par hectare)
         C_ENTREE_CULTURE_ha_Parc(SP)= sum(SC$Assol_Parc(SP,SC), Q_RES_Cult(SC) * Data_cult("CARB",SC) * Data_Cult("HRES",SC));

*XXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXX ENTREES DE CARBONE PAR AMENDEMENTS XXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXX
*ooooooo calcul de la quantité de carbone par culture et par hectare apportée par les amendements organiques (en tonnes par hectare)
         APP_AMDT_OTK_Cult(SC,SK)= Data_OTK(SK,"DOSE") * Data_OTK(SK,"HUM")* Data_OTK(SK,"CARB")* Data_OTK(SK,"FHUM") *  Matrice_OTK_Cult(SK,SC);
         APP_AMDT_Cult(SC)=sum(SK, APP_AMDT_OTK_Cult(SC,SK));
*ooooooo calcul de la quantité de carbone apportée par les amendements à la parcelle (en tonnes par hectare)
         C_ENTREE_AMDT_ha_Parc(SP) = sum(SC$Assol_Parc(SP,SC), APP_AMDT_Cult(SC));
         C_ENTREE_TOT_ha_Parc(SP)= C_ENTREE_CULTURE_ha_Parc(SP) + C_ENTREE_AMDT_ha_Parc(SP) ;
         C_ENTREE_TOT_Parc(SP)= C_ENTREE_TOT_ha_Parc(SP)*sum(SC, ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)) ;
TOTOS=1;
*XXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXX SORTIES DE CARBONE PAR MINERALISATION XXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXX
DISPLAY TOTOS;
*ooooooo AUTRES ANNEES - calcul de la quantité de carbone sortie par hectare en fonction de la Quantité de Carbone du sol (en tonnes par hectare) et de la nature du sol
                  LOOP(SP,
                          IF (Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"TYPE_SOL")=1,
                                  C_SORTIE_ha_Parc(SP) =  C_ORG_ha_Parc(SP) * Data_Sol("KAER","VERTISOL")* sum(SC$Assol_Parc(SP,SC), Data_Cult("KCROP",SC)) ;
                          );
                          IF (Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"TYPE_SOL")=2,
                                  C_SORTIE_ha_Parc(SP) =  C_ORG_ha_Parc(SP) * Data_Sol("KAER","FERRALSOL")* sum(SC$Assol_Parc(SP,SC), Data_Cult("KCROP",SC)) ;
                          );
                          IF (Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"TYPE_SOL")=3,
                                  C_SORTIE_ha_Parc(SP) =  C_ORG_ha_Parc(SP) * Data_Sol("KAER","ANDOSOL") * sum(SC$Assol_Parc(SP,SC), Data_Cult("KCROP",SC)) ;
                          );
                          IF (Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"TYPE_SOL")=4,
                                  C_SORTIE_ha_Parc(SP) =  C_ORG_ha_Parc(SP) * Data_Sol("KAER","NITISOL") * sum(SC$Assol_Parc(SP,SC), Data_Cult("KCROP",SC)) ;
                          );
                          IF (Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"TYPE_SOL")=5,
                                  C_SORTIE_ha_Parc(SP) =  C_ORG_ha_Parc(SP) * Data_Sol("KAER","AUTRES") * sum(SC$Assol_Parc(SP,SC), Data_Cult("KCROP",SC)) ;
                          );
                  );
**ooooooo AUTRES ANNEEs - calcul de la quantité de carbone sortie par parcelle (prise en compte de la surface de la parcelle) (en tonnes par parcelle)
                  C_SORTIE_Parc(SP) = C_SORTIE_ha_Parc(SP)*sum(SC, ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC));
                  C_ORG_ha_Parc(SP)= C_ORG_ha_Parc(SP) + (C_ENTREE_TOT_ha_Parc(SP) - C_SORTIE_ha_Parc(SP));
                  C_ORG_Parc(SP)= C_ORG_ha_Parc(SP)* sum(SC, ASSOL_Parc(SP,SC)) ;
         );

         STOCK_C_ENTREE_CULTURE_ha_Parc(SH,SP)= C_ENTREE_CULTURE_ha_Parc(SP);
         STOCK_C_ENTREE_AMDT_ha_Parc(SH,SP)= C_ENTREE_AMDT_ha_Parc(SP);
         STOCK_C_ENTREE_TOT_ha_Parc(SH,SP)= C_ENTREE_TOT_ha_Parc(SP);
         STOCK_C_ENTREE_TOT_Parc(SH,SP)= C_ENTREE_TOT_Parc(SP);
         STOCK_C_SORTIE_ha_Parc(SH,SP)= C_SORTIE_ha_Parc(SP);
         STOCK_C_SORTIE_Parc(SH,SP)= C_SORTIE_Parc(SP);
         STOCK_C_ORG_Parc(SH,SP)= C_ORG_Parc(SP);
         STOCK_C_ORG_ha_Parc(SH,SP)= C_ORG_ha_Parc(SP);
* Ici on calcul de la pourcentage de MO avec la quantité de carbone obtenue après chaque culture divisée par le produit de la quantité
* totale de sol multiplé par la densité, la profondeur et le tout multiplié par 100 por obtenir un pourcentage

         LOOP(SP,
                 IF (Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"TYPE_SOL")=1,
                         MAT_ORG_Parc(SP) = C_ORG_Parc(SP) / (10000 * Data_Sol("DENS","VERTISOL") * Data_Sol("PROF","VERTISOL"))  * 100 ;
                 );
                 IF (Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"TYPE_SOL")=2,
                         MAT_ORG_Parc(SP) = C_ORG_Parc(SP) / (10000 * Data_Sol("DENS","FERRALSOL")* Data_Sol("PROF","FERRALSOL")) * 100 ;
                 );
                 IF (Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"TYPE_SOL")=3,
                         MAT_ORG_Parc(SP) = C_ORG_Parc(SP) / (10000 * Data_Sol("DENS","ANDOSOL")  * Data_Sol("PROF","ANDOSOL"))   * 100 ;
                 );
                 IF (Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"TYPE_SOL")=4,
                         MAT_ORG_Parc(SP) = C_ORG_Parc(SP) / (10000 * Data_Sol("DENS","NITISOL")  * Data_Sol("PROF","NITISOL"))   * 100 ;
                 );
                 IF (Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"TYPE_SOL")=5,
                         MAT_ORG_Parc(SP) = C_ORG_Parc(SP) / (10000 * Data_Sol("DENS","AUTRES")   * Data_Sol("PROF","AUTRES"))    * 100 ;
                 );
         );

         STOCK_MAT_ORG_Parc(SH,SP)= MAT_ORG_Parc(SP);

*MMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMM
*MMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMM WISORCH MMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMM
*MMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMM

* Calcul du niveau de drainage d'eau par parcelle exprimé en - mm d'eau.an-1
         DRAI_Parc(SP)= Data_parc_Gwad(SP,"PLUVIO_PARC")/3 ;
         STOCK_DRAI_Parc(SH,SP)=DRAI_Parc(SP);
* Calcul du niveau initial de chordécone exprimé en - kg de CLD.ha-1

         CLD_ha_Parc_init(SP) =  Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"CLD_KG_HA");
         STOCK_CLD_ha_Parc_init(SH,SP)= CLD_ha_Parc_init(SP);

         CLD_Parc_init(SP) = CLD_ha_Parc_init(SP)*Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"SURF_HA");
         STOCK_CLD_Parc_init(SH,SP) = CLD_Parc_init(SP);

         IF( NB_BOUCLE<=2,
                 CLD_Cont_ha_Parc(SP)=CLD_Ha_Parc_init(SP);

         ELSE

                  LOOP(SP,
                          IF (Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"TYPE_SOL")=1,
                                  CLD_Cont_ha_Parc(SP)$Data_Sol("KOC","VERTISOL") = CLD_Cont_ha_Parc(SP)*exp((-10/(Data_Sol("KOC","VERTISOL")*C_ORG_ha_Parc(SP))*(DRAI_Parc(SP)/1000)));
                          );
                          IF (Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"TYPE_SOL")=2,
                                  CLD_Cont_ha_Parc(SP)$Data_Sol("KOC","FERRALSOL") = CLD_Cont_ha_Parc(SP)*exp((-10/(Data_Sol("KOC","FERRALSOL")*C_ORG_ha_Parc(SP))*(DRAI_Parc(SP)/1000)));
                          );
                          IF (Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"TYPE_SOL")=3,
                                  CLD_Cont_ha_Parc(SP)$Data_Sol("KOC","ANDOSOL") = CLD_Cont_ha_Parc(SP)*exp((-10/(Data_Sol("KOC","ANDOSOL")*C_ORG_ha_Parc(SP))*(DRAI_Parc(SP)/1000)));
                          );
                          IF (Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"TYPE_SOL")=4,
                                  CLD_Cont_ha_Parc(SP)$Data_Sol("KOC","NITISOL") = CLD_Cont_ha_Parc(SP)*exp((-10/(Data_Sol("KOC","NITISOL")*C_ORG_ha_Parc(SP))*(DRAI_Parc(SP)/1000)));
                          );
                          IF (Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"TYPE_SOL")=5,
                                  CLD_Cont_ha_Parc(SP)$Data_Sol("KOC","AUTRES") = CLD_Cont_ha_Parc(SP)*exp((-10/(Data_Sol("KOC","AUTRES")*C_ORG_ha_Parc(SP))*(DRAI_Parc(SP)/1000)));
                          );
                  );
         );

         STOCK_CLD_Cont_ha_Parc(SH,SP) = CLD_Cont_ha_Parc(SP);

         CLD_Cont_Parc(SP)=CLD_Cont_ha_Parc(SP)*Data_Parc_Gwad(SP,"SURF_HA");
         STOCK_CLD_Cont_Parc(SH,SP) = CLD_Cont_Parc(SP);

         IF (NB_BOUCLE>3,
                  DIFF_CLD(SH,SP) = STOCK_CLD_cont_Parc(SH,SP) - STOCK_CLD_cont_Parc(SH-1,SP) ;
         );
*MMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMM
*MMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMM FIN MORGWANIK & WISORCH MMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMM
*MMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMM







*ooooooo Calcul des indicateurs d'évaluation ooooooooooooooooooooooooooooooooooo
         EVAL_ELEC_Gwad  $STOCK_ELEC_Gwad(SH-1) = STOCK_ELEC_Gwad(SH) / STOCK_ELEC_Gwad(SH-1);
         STOCK_EVAL_ELEC_Gwad(SH)  = EVAL_ELEC_Gwad;

         EVAL_REV_Gwad   $STOCK_REV_Gwad(SH-1)  = STOCK_REV_Gwad(SH)  / STOCK_REV_Gwad(SH-1);
         STOCK_EVAL_REV_Gwad(SH)   = EVAL_REV_Gwad;

         EVAL_IFT_Gwad   $STOCK_IFT_Gwad(SH-1)  = STOCK_IFT_Gwad(SH)  / STOCK_IFT_Gwad(SH-1);
         STOCK_EVAL_IFT_Gwad(SH)   = EVAL_IFT_Gwad;

         EVAL_MO_Gwad    $STOCK_MO_Gwad(SH-1)   = STOCK_MO_Gwad(SH)   / STOCK_MO_Gwad(SH-1);
         STOCK_EVAL_MO_Gwad(SH)    = EVAL_MO_Gwad;

         EVAL_GES_Gwad   $STOCK_GES_Gwad(SH-1)  = STOCK_GES_Gwad(SH)  / STOCK_GES_Gwad(SH-1);
         STOCK_EVAL_GES_Gwad(SH)   = EVAL_GES_Gwad;

         EVAL_AZOTE_Gwad $STOCK_AZOTE_Gwad(SH-1)= STOCK_AZOTE_Gwad(SH)/ STOCK_AZOTE_Gwad(SH-1);
         STOCK_EVAL_AZOTE_Gwad(SH) = EVAL_AZOTE_Gwad;


DISPLAY ASSOL_CPT;

NB_BOUCLE=NB_BOUCLE+1;
);






PAD_SOBT_TOTAL$STOCK_ASSOL_SOBT_tot_TOTAL("init") = ABS( STOCK_ASSOL_SOBT_tot_TOTAL("calib") - STOCK_ASSOL_SOBT_tot_TOTAL("init") )/ STOCK_ASSOL_SOBT_tot_TOTAL("init");
PAD_SEBT_TOTAL$STOCK_ASSOL_SEBT_tot_TOTAL("init") = ABS( STOCK_ASSOL_SEBT_tot_TOTAL("calib") - STOCK_ASSOL_SEBT_tot_TOTAL("init") )/ STOCK_ASSOL_SEBT_tot_TOTAL("init");
PAD_NBT_TOTAL $STOCK_ASSOL_NBT_tot_TOTAL("init")  = ABS( STOCK_ASSOL_NBT_tot_TOTAL("calib")  - STOCK_ASSOL_NBT_tot_TOTAL("init")  )/ STOCK_ASSOL_NBT_tot_TOTAL("init") ;
PAD_NGT_TOTAL $STOCK_ASSOL_NGT_tot_TOTAL("init")  = ABS( STOCK_ASSOL_NGT_tot_TOTAL("calib")  - STOCK_ASSOL_NGT_tot_TOTAL("init")  )/ STOCK_ASSOL_NGT_tot_TOTAL("init") ;
PAD_CGT_TOTAL $STOCK_ASSOL_CGT_tot_TOTAL("init")  = ABS( STOCK_ASSOL_CGT_tot_TOTAL("calib")  - STOCK_ASSOL_CGT_tot_TOTAL("init")  )/ STOCK_ASSOL_CGT_tot_TOTAL("init") ;
PAD_EGT_TOTAL $STOCK_ASSOL_EGT_tot_TOTAL("init")  = ABS( STOCK_ASSOL_EGT_tot_TOTAL("calib")  - STOCK_ASSOL_EGT_tot_TOTAL("init")  )/ STOCK_ASSOL_EGT_tot_TOTAL("init") ;
PAD_MG_TOTAL  $STOCK_ASSOL_MG_tot_TOTAL("init")   = ABS( STOCK_ASSOL_MG_tot_TOTAL("calib")   - STOCK_ASSOL_MG_tot_TOTAL("init")   )/ STOCK_ASSOL_MG_tot_TOTAL("init")  ;
PAD_GWAD_TOTAL$STOCK_ASSOL_GWAD_tot_TOTAL("init") = ABS( STOCK_ASSOL_GWAD_tot_TOTAL("calib") - STOCK_ASSOL_GWAD_tot_TOTAL("init") )/ STOCK_ASSOL_GWAD_tot_TOTAL("init");

PAD_SOBT_ANA$STOCK_ASSOL_SOBT_tot_ANA("init") = ABS( STOCK_ASSOL_SOBT_tot_ANA("calib") - STOCK_ASSOL_SOBT_tot_ANA("init") )/ STOCK_ASSOL_SOBT_tot_ANA("init");
PAD_SEBT_ANA$STOCK_ASSOL_SEBT_tot_ANA("init") = ABS( STOCK_ASSOL_SEBT_tot_ANA("calib") - STOCK_ASSOL_SEBT_tot_ANA("init") )/ STOCK_ASSOL_SEBT_tot_ANA("init");
PAD_NBT_ANA $STOCK_ASSOL_NBT_tot_ANA("init")  = ABS( STOCK_ASSOL_NBT_tot_ANA("calib")  - STOCK_ASSOL_NBT_tot_ANA("init")  )/ STOCK_ASSOL_NBT_tot_ANA("init") ;
PAD_NGT_ANA $STOCK_ASSOL_NGT_tot_ANA("init")  = ABS( STOCK_ASSOL_NGT_tot_ANA("calib")  - STOCK_ASSOL_NGT_tot_ANA("init")  )/ STOCK_ASSOL_NGT_tot_ANA("init") ;
PAD_CGT_ANA $STOCK_ASSOL_CGT_tot_ANA("init")  = ABS( STOCK_ASSOL_CGT_tot_ANA("calib")  - STOCK_ASSOL_CGT_tot_ANA("init")  )/ STOCK_ASSOL_CGT_tot_ANA("init") ;
PAD_EGT_ANA $STOCK_ASSOL_EGT_tot_ANA("init")  = ABS( STOCK_ASSOL_EGT_tot_ANA("calib")  - STOCK_ASSOL_EGT_tot_ANA("init")  )/ STOCK_ASSOL_EGT_tot_ANA("init") ;
PAD_MG_ANA  $STOCK_ASSOL_MG_tot_ANA("init")   = ABS( STOCK_ASSOL_MG_tot_ANA("calib")   - STOCK_ASSOL_MG_tot_ANA("init")   )/ STOCK_ASSOL_MG_tot_ANA("init")  ;
PAD_GWAD_ANA$STOCK_ASSOL_GWAD_tot_ANA("init") = ABS( STOCK_ASSOL_GWAD_tot_ANA("calib") - STOCK_ASSOL_GWAD_tot_ANA("init") )/ STOCK_ASSOL_GWAD_tot_ANA("init");

PAD_SOBT_BAN$STOCK_ASSOL_SOBT_tot_BAN("init") = ABS( STOCK_ASSOL_SOBT_tot_BAN("calib") - STOCK_ASSOL_SOBT_tot_BAN("init") )/ STOCK_ASSOL_SOBT_tot_BAN("init");
PAD_SEBT_BAN$STOCK_ASSOL_SEBT_tot_BAN("init") = ABS( STOCK_ASSOL_SEBT_tot_BAN("calib") - STOCK_ASSOL_SEBT_tot_BAN("init") )/ STOCK_ASSOL_SEBT_tot_BAN("init");
PAD_NBT_BAN $STOCK_ASSOL_NBT_tot_BAN("init")  = ABS( STOCK_ASSOL_NBT_tot_BAN("calib")  - STOCK_ASSOL_NBT_tot_BAN("init")  )/ STOCK_ASSOL_NBT_tot_BAN("init") ;
PAD_NGT_BAN $STOCK_ASSOL_NGT_tot_BAN("init")  = ABS( STOCK_ASSOL_NGT_tot_BAN("calib")  - STOCK_ASSOL_NGT_tot_BAN("init")  )/ STOCK_ASSOL_NGT_tot_BAN("init") ;
PAD_CGT_BAN $STOCK_ASSOL_CGT_tot_BAN("init")  = ABS( STOCK_ASSOL_CGT_tot_BAN("calib")  - STOCK_ASSOL_CGT_tot_BAN("init")  )/ STOCK_ASSOL_CGT_tot_BAN("init") ;
PAD_EGT_BAN $STOCK_ASSOL_EGT_tot_BAN("init")  = ABS( STOCK_ASSOL_EGT_tot_BAN("calib")  - STOCK_ASSOL_EGT_tot_BAN("init")  )/ STOCK_ASSOL_EGT_tot_BAN("init") ;
PAD_MG_BAN  $STOCK_ASSOL_MG_tot_BAN("init")   = ABS( STOCK_ASSOL_MG_tot_BAN("calib")   - STOCK_ASSOL_MG_tot_BAN("init")   )/ STOCK_ASSOL_MG_tot_BAN("init")  ;
PAD_GWAD_BAN$STOCK_ASSOL_GWAD_tot_BAN("init") = ABS( STOCK_ASSOL_GWAD_tot_BAN("calib") - STOCK_ASSOL_GWAD_tot_BAN("init") )/ STOCK_ASSOL_GWAD_tot_BAN("init");

PAD_SOBT_BAN_EX$STOCK_ASSOL_SOBT_tot_BAN_EX("init") = ABS( STOCK_ASSOL_SOBT_tot_BAN_EX("calib") - STOCK_ASSOL_SOBT_tot_BAN_EX("init") )/ STOCK_ASSOL_SOBT_tot_BAN_EX("init");
PAD_SEBT_BAN_EX$STOCK_ASSOL_SEBT_tot_BAN_EX("init") = ABS( STOCK_ASSOL_SEBT_tot_BAN_EX("calib") - STOCK_ASSOL_SEBT_tot_BAN_EX("init") )/ STOCK_ASSOL_SEBT_tot_BAN_EX("init");
PAD_NBT_BAN_EX $STOCK_ASSOL_NBT_tot_BAN_EX("init")  = ABS( STOCK_ASSOL_NBT_tot_BAN_EX("calib")  - STOCK_ASSOL_NBT_tot_BAN_EX("init")  )/ STOCK_ASSOL_NBT_tot_BAN_EX("init") ;
